# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Tswana translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Molao Motheo Wa Zimbabwe (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

MOLAO

Go ntsha le go fetola Molao Motheo wa Zimbabwe. O DUMETSWE ke Tautona le Palamente ya Zimbabwe. 1

Sethlogo se se khutshwane Molao o o ka nna Molao Motheo o musha wa Zimbabwe (No. 20) Molao, 2013.



Tlhaloso Mo Molaong oo – “molao motheo wa pele” o raya gore molao wa Zimbabwe o ne o dirisiwa go tswa ka letsatsi la 18th Moranang 1980 go fitlha o fetolwa; “molao motheo o musha” go raya molao wa Zimbabwe o musha o tlhomamisiweng mo Thulaganyong ee.



Go ntshewa kwa Molao Motheo wa pele ke Molao Motheo o musha

(1) Fa go tirisiwa mophatonyana wa bobedi (2), mo molao wa pele o tla fetolwa ke Molao o musha.

(2) Gore batho ba se ke ba ngongorega go a tlhalosiwa go re— 15

(a)

Molao o musha o tla dumelwa go tswa ka le tsatsi o kwalwa se se tlhalosiwang ke Rulaganyo ya Borataro e tswang mo Molaong Motheo o ne ka gore tsatsi lena Mokwalo o o kwadilwe mo pampering e tswa mo karolwaneng ya masome a matlhano le ngwe ya Molao wa pele; le

(b) Go tswa mo le tsatseng la go dumela Molao o musha se se tlhalosiwang ke temana e ko godimo (a), Molao wa pele o tla nna molao tota ka ga go tlhalosetsweng ke karolo ya borataro ya Molao Motheo o musha. Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

## Section 34

Maikaelelo a go thusa Lefatshe le maphata otlhe a goromente. Mmuso o siame. Popagano ya Setshaba, kagiso le tshiamo. Go godisa bothlokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololesego ya batho. Megopolo ka mafatshe a sele. Tlhabologo ya Setshaba. Go fa bokgoni le go thama ditiro. Dijo. Setso. Tekatekanyo ya bong. Go babalesega mo dikgaolong tsotlhe tsa Lefatshe. Bana. Bonana. Bagolo. Batho ba banang le bogole. Mabutswapele a ntwa ya kgololesego. Tiro le bo tsalano ko tirong. Tshireletso ya lelwapa. Lenyalo. Dithuto. Lefelo la tshireletso. Manaeo a botsogo. Kgathalelo ya setshaba. Thuso ya ka fa molaong. Metshameko le ditlamelo tse di tlosang bodutu. Tshomarelo ya kitso ya tlholego. Go amogela melao ya mafatshe a sele mo lefatsheng.

KGAOLO 3 BOAGEDI

## Section 35

## Section 40

## Section 43

Boagedi jwa Zimbabwe. Boagedi ka matsalo. Boagedi ka losika. Boagedi jwa kwadiso. Go ntshewa jwa boagedi. Go amogela boagedi ka nyalo. Lephata la boagedi le go ya ko mafatsheng a sele. Maatla a Palamente a akanyetsa boagedi. Tswelediso le puseletso ya boagedi jwa pele.



KGAOLO 4 Kitsiso Ya Ditshwanelo KAROLO 1


Tiriso Le Tlhaloso Ya Kgaolo Ya Bonne

## Section 44

## Section 45

## Section 46

## Section 47

Tlamego ya go tlhompha ditshwanelo le kgololesego ya batho. Tiriso ya Kgaolo 4. Tlhaloso ya Kgaolo 4. Kgaolo 4 ga a tshwanelela go kgoreletsa dingwe ditshwanelo.


KAROLO 2 BoTlhokwa Jwa Tshwanelo le Kgololesego Ya Batho

## Section 68

Ditshwanelo tsa botshelo. Ditshwanelo tsa kgololesego ya batho. Ditshwanelo tsa go tshwarwa le go tlhatlhelwa. Ditshwanelo tsa seriti sa motho. Ditshwanelo tsa tshireletso ya motho a ikemetsi kanosi. Kgololesego mo go kgokgontsweng, nyenyafatso kana tlontlololo kana kotlhatswa. Kgololesego mo go go bantshweng. Kgololesego mo go patlelediweng tiro Tekatekano le go kgethelola ope. Tshwanelo ya go nna nosi. Kgololesego ya kgoboganyo le kgolagano. Kgololesego ya go supegetsa le go kopa mokwalo wa se mmuso. Kgololesego ya letswalo. Kgololesego mo mokgweng wa go bua le kgololego ya go tsamaisa mafoko. Tshwanelo ya go tlhola kitso. Puo le setso. Kgololesego ya tiro,kgwebo le go iphithisa nako. Ditshwanelo tsa badiri. Kgololesego ya go tsamaya le bonno. Ditshwanelo tsa sepolotiki. Tshwanelo ya tsamaiso ya molao. 4



Mophato

## Section 69

Tshwanelo ya go reediwa mo ko lekaneng.

## Section 70

Ditshwanelo tsa batho ba ba latofetsweng.

## Section 71

Ditshwanelo tsa kago kgotsa le lefatshe la mongwe.

## Section 72

Ditshwanelo tsa lefatshe la temo thuo.

## Section 73

Ditshwanelo tsa tikologo.

## Section 74

Kgololesego mo go sena lenaneo la teleko.

Tshwanelo ya thuto.

## Section 76

Tshwanelo ya go kgathalela botsogo.

## Section 77

Tshwanelo ya dijo le metsi.

## Section 78

Ditshwanelo tsa go nyala. KAROLO 3 Tlhaloso ya Di Tshwanelo Dingwe

Tiriso ya Karolo 3. Ditshwanelo tsa basadi. Ditshwanelo tsa bana. Ditshwanelo tsa bagolo. Ditshwanelo tsa batho ba ba nang le bogole. Ditshwanelo tsa mabutswapele a ntwa ya kgololesego. KAROLO 4 Kgatelelo Ya BoTlhokwa Jwa Tshwanelo le Kgololesego Ya Batho

## Section 85

Kgatelelo ya botlhokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololesego ya batho. Karolo 5 Sekganedi se se Botlhokwa Mo Tshwanelong le Kgololesegong Ya Batho

## Section 86

## Section 87

Selekanyo sa ditshwanelo le kgololesego. Selekanyo fa sengwe sentse se tsweletse mo tshoganetsong ya morafe. KGAOLO 5 Bookamedi KAROLO 1 Taolo Ya Bookamedi

## Section 88

Taolo ya bookamedi. KAROLO 2

## Section 94 — Tautona Le Bathusa-Tautona

Tautona. Tiro ya ga Tautona. Matshwanedi a go tlhophiwa o le Tautona le Mothusa-Tautona. Tlhopho ya ga Tautona le Bathusa-Tautona. Kgoreletso mo go tlhopeng Tautona. Tshimologo ya tiro mo ofising ya ga Tautona le mo ofising ya Bathusa-Tautona.

Mophato

## Section 95

## Section 96

## Section 97

## Section 98

## Section 99

## Section 100

## Section 101

## Section 102

## Section 103

## Section 104

## Section 105

## Section 106

## Section 107

## Section 108

## Section 109

Lobaka lwa ga Tautona le Bathusa-Tautona mo ofising. Go athola marapo kwa ga Tautona kana Mothusa-Tautona. Tloso ya ga Tautona kgotsa Mothusa-Tautona ko ofising. Tshireletsego ya ga Tautona. Tiro ya Bathusa-Tautona Moemedi wa ga Tautona. Tlhatlhamo ya setilo sa ga Tautona kgotsa Mothusa-Tautona ka mabaka a tiragalo e tshwana le leso kgotsa go athola marapo kana go sa kgoni tiro. Tuelo ya ga Tautona le Bathusa-Tautona. Tautona, Bathusa-Tautona le batshwara ofisi ba pele ga ba tshwanela go tshwara engwe ofisi kana tiro. Tlhopho ya Matona le Bathusa -Matona. Khuduthamaga ya puso. Maitshwaro a Bathusa-Tautona, Matona le Bathusa Matona. Boikarabelo jwa Bathusa-Tautona, Matona le Bathusa Matona. Lobaka lwa Matona le Bathusa-Matona. Go tlhoka-bokgoni mo go Goromente. KAROLO 4 TIRO YA BOOKAMEDI

## Section 110

## Section 111

## Section 112

## Section 113

Tiro ya bookamedi ba ga Tautona le khuduthamaga. Ntwa le kagiso. Maatla a mosa. Maemo a tshoganetso setshaba. KAROLO 5 Mmueledi-Mogolo

## Section 114

## Section 115

Mmueledi-Mogolo. Tloso ya ga Mmueledi-Mogolo ko ofising. KGAOLO 6 Molao Motheo KAROLO 1 Taolo Ya Molao Motheo

## Section 116

## Section 117

Melao. Pupo le bogolo jwa taolo ya Kgotla ya Molao. KAROLO 2 Palamente

## Section 118

## Section 119

Palamente. Seabe sa Palamente.

KAROLO 3 Matona A Senetha Mophato

## Section 120

## Section 121

## Section 122

## Section 123

Popego ya Senethe. Matshwanedi le mo go sa tlhokegeng mo mothong go tlhophiwa jaaka le loko la Senethe. Tautona wa Senethe. Mothusa-Tautona wa Senethe. KAROLO 4 Phutego Ya Setshaba

## Section 124

## Section 125

## Section 126

## Section 127

Popego ya Phuthego Setshaba. Matshwanedi le mo go sa tlhokegeng go tlhophiwa go emela Phuthego Setshaba. Sebui sa Phutego Setshaba. Mothusa-Sebui wa Phuthego Setshaba. KAROLO 5 Tloso Ya Mapalamente

## Section 128

## Section 129

Maikano a Mopalamente. Lobaka lwa setilo sa Mopalamente ko ofising. KAROLO 6 Molao Le Mangwe Maatla

## Section 130

## Section 131

## Section 132

## Section 133

## Section 134

Maatla a molao le tiro ya Senathe le Batsenelela Phutego ya Setshaba. Melao ya Palamente le tsamaiso ya go idumela. Tshimololo ya Melao ya Palamente. Go amogela Melao ya Palamente Mangwe maatla a molao. KAROLO 7 Tsamaiso Ya Palamente

## Section 135

## Section 136

## Section 137

## Section 138

## Section 139

## Section 140

## Section 141

## Section 142

Mogolwane wa Palamente. Batho ba ba taelang ko Palamente. Palo ya batho ko Palamenteng. Tlhopho le tshwanelo ya ba reetse ko Palamenteng. Manno a Tlhomaganyo. Puo le melaetsa ya ga Tautona ko Palamenteng. Go amana le Palamente le seabe sa morafe ko Palamenteng. Tetlelelo ya ditsheko ko Palamenteng. KAROLO 8 Sebaka, Phatlalatso Le Kopanyo Ya Palamente

## Section 143

Sebaka le Phatlhalatso ya Palamente.

Mophato

## Section 144

## Section 145

## Section 146

## Section 147

Ditlhopho tse di golwane morago ga go phatlhalatsa Palamente. Phuthego ya ntlha ya Palamente morago ga di tlhopho tse di golwane. Phuthego le nako ya go ikhutsa. Phetho ya Melawana, mogopolo, kopo le tse dingwe dikgang ka go phatlhalatsa palamente. KAROLO 9 Dingwe Di Kgang Tse Di Tsamaelanang Le Palamente

## Section 148

## Section 149

## Section 150

## Section 151

## Section 152

## Section 153

## Section 154

Tlotlo le tshireletso ya Palamente. Tshwanelo ya go kopa ka mokwalo wa semmuso mo Palamenteng. Lefelo la Palamente. Komiti ya Manno ya Melawana le Tlhomaganyo. Komiti ya Molao ya Palamente. Go duelwa kwa ga Tautona wa Senetha, Sebui sa Palamente le Mapalamente. Mokwaledi wa Palamente le ba bangwe babereki. KGAOLO 7 Ditlhopho KAROLO 1 Tsamaiso Le Thomagano Ya Ditlhopho

## Section 155

## Section 156

## Section 157

Tsamaiso ya ditiragalo tsa dithopho. Go dira ditlhopho le ditlhopho tsa dipotso. Molao wa Ditlhopho. KAROLO 2 Nako Ya Ditlhopho

## Section 158

## Section 159

Nako ya ditlhopho. Go tswala diphatlha mo ditlhophong. KAROLO 3 Go Tshwaya Seelo Sa Botlhophi

## Section 160

## Section 161

Palo ya dikgaolo le dikgotla. Go tshwaya seelo sa dikgaolo tsa botlhophi. KGAOLO 8 Boatlhodi Le Dikgotla KAROLO 1 Tsamaiso Ya Dikgotla

## Section 162

## Section 163

## Section 164

Taolo ya boatlhodi. Boatlhodi. Boatlhodi jo bo ikemetseng ka nosi.

Mophato

## Section 165

## Section 166

## Section 167

## Section 168

## Section 169

## Section 170

## Section 171

## Section 172

## Section 173

## Section 174

## Section 175

## Section 176

Tsamaiso ya boatlhodi. Kgotla ya Molao Motheo. Maatla a Kgotla ya Molao Motheo Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa. Basekisi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa.. Kgotla Kgolo. Basekisi ba Kgotla Kgolo. Kgotla ya Babereki. Kgotla la Tsamaiso. Dingwe dikgotla le Lekgotla le le hapegileng Maatla a dikgotla mo dikgangeng tsa Molao Motheo. Maatla magolo a Kgotla ya Molao Motheo, Kgotla e Botlhokwatlokwa le Kgotla Kgolo KAROLO 2 TLHOPO LE TLOSO YA BAATHLODI

## Section 177

## Section 178

## Section 179

## Section 180

## Section 181

## Section 182

## Section 183

## Section 184

## Section 185

## Section 186

## Section 187

Ditlhoko tsa baathlodi ba Lekgotla la Molao Motheo. Ditlhoko tsa baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Botlhokwa. Ditlhoko tsa baatlhodi ba Kgotla Kgolo, Kgotla ya babereki le Lekgotla la di Tsamaisa-tiro. Go tlhophiwa ga baatlhodi. Ditlhopho tsa baetsaboatlhodi. Go tlhophiwa ga bo mmakasetarata le maloko a baatlhodi. Baatlhodi ba tshwanetse go tlhophiwa mo kgotleng engwe fela. Dikamano tsa baatlhodi go bonagatsa setshaba. Boikarabelo. Sebaka sa Bolaodi. Maemo le sebaka sa tiro ya baatlhodi. KAROLO 3 Kgotla Ya Boatlhodi

## Section 189

## Section 190

## Section 191

Tshimologo le popego ya le phata la tsa Semolao. Ditiro tsa le phata la tsa Semolao. Go ikanyega le go nna pepene ga di thulaganyo tsa ba le phata la tsa Semolao. KAROLO 4 Dino Tse Di Amana leMotho Otlhe Fela

## Section 192

## Section 193

Molao o tshwanetse go dirisiwa. Le phata la ditsheko tsa tshenyo. KGAOLO 9 Tsamaiso Ya Mookamedi Wa Morafe Le Boeteledipele

## Section 194

## Section 195

## Section 196

Makaedi a botlhokwa le boeteledipele jwa puso le tsamaiso. Tsamaiso ya thekiso le ikhonomi ke Lefatshe. Maikarabelo a badiredi kgotsa babereki ba setshaba le tsamaiso ya boeteledipele.

## Section 197

## Section 198

Mabaka a fiwang di tlhoko tsa semmuso/goromente tse dilaolwang. Molao Motheo o gatelelang Kgaolo 9. KGAOLO 10 Bodirelapuso

## Section 199

## Section 200

## Section 201

## Section 202

## Section 203

## Section 204

## Section 205

Bodirelapuso. Goitshwara ga maloko a bodirelapuso. Tona ya Badirelapuso. Madirelo le thamo tsa ba direlapuso ba kgotla la ba sekaseki. Tiro ya badirelapuso. Baemedi ba ba botlhokwa mo Zimbabwe. Bakwaledi ba sennelaruri. KGAOLO 11 Tshireletso KAROLO 1 Dingwe Dino Fela

## Section 206

## Section 207

## Section 208

## Section 209

## Section 210

Tshireletso setshaba. Bodiredi jwa tshireletso. Maitshwaro a maloko a bodiredi jwa tshireletso ya setshaba. Khansele ya tshireletso setshaba. Mekgwa ya ngongorego. KAROLO 2 Lephata La Masole

## Section 211

## Section 212

## Section 213

## Section 214

## Section 215

## Section 216

## Section 217

## Section 218

Masole. Tiro ya Masole. Mafelo a tiro a Sesole. Maikarabelo a Sesole mo sepolotikeng. Tona o na le boikarabelo mo Setlhopheng sa Masole. Taola ka Masole a tshireletso. Tshimolodiso le thamo ya lekgotla la basekisi ba Sesole. Tiro ya Lekgotla la Sesole. KAROLO 3 Lekgotla La Mapodisi

## Section 219

## Section 220

## Section 221

## Section 222

## Section 223

Bodiedi jwa Sepodisi. Tona wa Sepodisi. Ramapodisi. Tshimologo le thamo ya Lekgotla la Mapodisi. Tiro ya Lekgotla la Mapodisi.

KAROLO 4 Setlhopha Sa Botlhali

## Section 224

## Section 225

## Section 226

Tshimologo ya Setlhopha sa Botlhali. Tona wa Setlhopha sa Botlhali. Taolo kgotsa laolo tsa bodiredi jwa botlhali ba setshaba. KAROLO 5 Kgolegelo Le Bodiredi Jwa Paakanyo

## Section 227

## Section 228

## Section 229

## Section 230

## Section 231

Kgolegelo le Bodiredi jwa Paakanyo le ditiro tsa yone. Tona e enang le maikarabelo mo bodireding jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo. Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng wa bodiredijwa Kgolegelo le paakanyo. Tshimologo le thamo ya Bodiredi le Kgolegelo. Tiro ya lekgotla la ba sekaseki ba bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le Paakanyo. KGAOLO 12 Makgotla A A Basekaseki A Ikemetseng Puso Ya Batho Ka Batho KAROLO 1 Go Sa Papamala

## Section 232

## Section 233

## Section 234

## Section 235

## Section 236

## Section 237

Dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse di ikemetseng. Maikaelelo a a dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse dikemetseng. Bodiredi jwa le kgotla la basekaseki jo bo ekemetseng. Lekgotla la basekaseki le ekemetseng. Maloko a a basekaseki ba e kemetseng ba ba seng ba sepolitiki. Tlhopho le tloso go tsweng mo ofising ya bo loko jo ekementseng. KAROLO 2 Lekgotla La Ditlhopho

## Section 238

## Section 239

## Section 240

## Section 241

Tshimolodiso le Thamo ya lekgotla la basekaseki la ditlhopho mo Zimbabwe. Tiro ya lekgotla la basekaseki la ditlhopho mo Zimbabwe. Go kogiwa mo kgaisanong le maemo a bodireding jwa ditlhopho jwa Zimbabwe. Lekgotla la basekaseki la ditlhopho la Zimbabwe go naya ipego ka ditlhopho le tsa patlo maikutlo. KAROLO 3 Lekgotla La Ditshwanelo Tsa Batho

## Section 242

## Section 243

## Section 244

Tshimologo le thamo ya lekgotla la basekaseki laditshwanelo tsa batho. Ditiro tsa lekgotla la basekaseki le ditshwanelo tsa setho mo Zimbabwe. Pego ga lekgotla la basekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho mo Zimbabwe. KAROLO 4 Lekgotla La Tekatekanyo Ya Bong

## Section 245

## Section 246

## Section 247

Tshimololo le popo ya Lekgotla la Tekatekanyo ya bong. Tiro ya lekgotla la basekaseki la bong jwa motho mo Zimbabwe. Pego go bapa le lekgotla la basekaseki jwa bong tsa Zimbabwe.

KAROLO 5 Lekgotla La Go Pega Le Go Tsamaisa Kitso Mophato

## Section 248

## Section 249

## Section 250 — Tshimologo le thamo ya lekgotla la basekaseki la tsamaiso dikgang ya

Zimbabwe. Tiro ya lekgotla la basekaseki la tsamaiso dikgang mo Zimbabwe. Pego ya lekgotla la basekaseki la tsamaiso dikgang mo Zimbabwe. KAROLO 6 Lekgotla La Kagiso Le Go Itshwarelana

## Section 251

## Section 252

## Section 253

Tshimolodiso le tlhamo ya Kagiso le Kagisano ya setshaba. Ditiro tsalekgotla la setshaba la basekaseki ba Kagiso le Kagisano. Pego tsa lekgotla la basekaseki ba Kagiso le Kagisanotsa setshaba. KGAOLO 13 Dikgotla Tsa Go Thibelela Tshenyetso Setshaba Le Borukhutlhi KAROLO 1 Lekgotla La Go Thibelela Tshenyetso Setshaba

## Section 254

## Section 255

## Section 256

## Section 257 — Tshimolodiso le dikarolo tsa lephata la twantso tshenyetsho setshaba la

Zimbabwe. Diro tsa lephata la twantso tshenyetsho setshaba la Zimbabwe. Tiriso ya ditlamelo dingwe tsa Kgaolo 12 go le phateng la twantso tshenyetsho setshaba la Zimbabwe. Dikitsiso ke ba lephata la twantso tshenyetsho setshaba la Zimbabwe. KAROLO 2 Taolo Ya Basekisi Ba Setshaba

## Section 258

## Section 259

## Section 260

## Section 261

## Section 262

## Section 263

Tshimologo le ditiro tsa Lephata la Bosekisi. Mosekisi Mogolo le ba bangwe. Kgololesego jwa Msekisi Mogolo. Boitshwaro jwa ba Lephata la Bosekisi. Mosekisi Mogolo o tshwanetse go fa pego ka tiro gangwe ko Palamenteng. Mangwe maatla tsheko. KGAOLO 14

Goromente Wa Dikgaolo Le Dikgotla Ketapele. KAROLO 1 MAITSENO

## Section 264

## Section 265

## Section 266

Go iketsetsa maatla a mmuso le boikarabelo. Ditsamaiso ka karetso tsa puso ya bontlha jwa lefatshe le puso ya selegae. Boitshwaro jwa babereki ba dipuso tsa puso ya bontlha jwa lefatshe le puso ya selegae.

KAROLO 2 Dikgaolo Le Makhanselara A Dikgaolo Le Toropo Mophato

## Section 267

## Section 268

## Section 269

## Section 270

## Section 271

## Section 272

## Section 273

Dipuso tsa bontlha jwa lefatshe le dikgaolo tsa Zimbabwe. Makhanselara a dikgaolo. Makhanselara a toropo. Tiro ya khansele ya buntlha jwa lefatshe le toropo. Dikomiti tsa puso ya selegae. Badulastilo ba khansele ya buntlha jwa lefastshe. Dino tse di amanang le buntlha jwa lefastshe le toropo. KAROLO 3 Goromente Wa Dikgotla

## Section 274

## Section 275

## Section 276

## Section 277

## Section 278

## Section 279

Mmuso wa selegae sa toropo. Mmuso wa selegae sa magaeng. Tiro ya mmuso wa selegae. Ditlhopho tsa mmuso wa selegae. Sebaka sa ditilo tsa maloko a mmuso wa selegae. Tsamaiso ya mmuso wa selegae. KGAOLO 15 Baeteledipele Ba Setso

## Section 280

## Section 281

## Section 282

## Section 283

## Section 284

## Section 285

## Section 286

## Section 287

Baeteledipele ba setso. Ditsamaiso tse di tlhokomelwang ke Baeteledipele ba setso. Tiro ya Baeteldipele ba setso. Go beiwa le tloso ya baeteledipele ba setso. Dikatso le dituelo tsa baeteledipele ba setso. Khansele ya Setshaba le buntlha jwa lefatshe la Dikgosi. Tiro ya Khansele ya Setshaba le phutego ya buntlha jwa lefatshe la Dikgosi Dikgosi. Botlhale le Twaelo ya Komiti. KGAOLO 16 Lefatshe La Temo-Thuo

## Section 288

## Section 289

## Section 297

Tlhaloso ya Kgaolo 16. Ditsamaiso tse di kaelang taolo ya mothaba wa temo. Tswelelopele ya ditokelo tsa puso ya mothaba wa temo-thuo. Tswelediso ya ditshwanelo ya banni ba mothaba wa temo-thuo. Tshireletso ya sebaka sa banni ba mothaba wa temo-thuo. Go aga sesha sa temo ke Lefatshe. Tsamaiso ya mothaba wa temo-thuo. Go phimola dikeledi tsa mothaba wa temo-thuo o tserweng. Pupo le thamo ya Komiti ya Mothaba ya Zimbabwe. Tiro ya Komiti ya Mothaba ya Zimbabwe.

Tiriso ya Karolo 3. Tse di akaretsang tsamiso tsa go tlhaloso Molao Motheo. Tlholoso. Kaelo ya Dikgaolo, mephato, jalo jalo. Mofoko a bongwe go akaretsa a ntsifatso, le vice versa. Ditafola le ditlhogo. Kaelo ya nako. Kaelo ya batshwara di ofisi Kaelo ya Palamente. Kgakololo le puisano. Tlhopha. Go ithola marapo. Go dirisa moletlo, jalo jalo. Fa motho o reywang gore a se motshwari wa ofisi. Setlhopha le ditlamorago tsa phatlha mo mmeleng ya molao motheo. Go sa dumelana ga ditemana tse di tshwaneng tsa Molaong Motheo. THULAGANYO YA NTLHA: Folaga ya Setshaba, Pina ya Setshaba, Sekano sa Setshaba le Setlanyo sa Setshaba. THUALGANYO YA BOBEDI: Selekanyo sa Ditshwanelo mo Tswelediso mo Tshoganetso Setshaba. THULAGANYO YA BORARO: Maikano le Netefatso. THULAGANYO YA BONNE: Se se batliwang go nna mo tlhophi. THULAGANYO YA BOTLHANO: Tsamaiso ya melawana le tse dingwe dikgang ko Palamenteng. THULAGANYO YA BORATARO: Go simolola go dirisa Molao-Motheo, phetogo ya dino le go boloka madi.

KETAPELE ________ Rona batho ba Zimbabwe, Re solofela go na le kgololego, tsamaiso ya molao, tekatekanyo le go gana puso ya bo kolone, kgethololo ka mmala le tsotlhe phenyo le kgokgontso ka go tshwaragana kwa rona ka mefuta e farologaneng. Re tsholetsa le galaletsa sebete sa bo rre le mme ba ba ineetseng botshelo jwa bonne mo ntweng ya kgololego, Re tlotla mabutswapele ka go mdira thata gore go nne le tswelelopele ya lefatshe la rona, Re lemoga puso ya batho ka batho, tshiamo, go direla mo pontshong, le boikarabelo jwa goromente le puso ka molao, Re rurifatsa maitlamo a rona a go godisa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho, Re leboga khumo ya tlholego ya lefatshe la rona, Ra itumela ngwao le setso sa rona, Re batla go tlosa dilo tsotlhe tse di kgoreletsang le tse di tshwenyang tswelelopele mo lefatsheng la rona, Re tumela kgololego, tekatekanyo, kagiso, tsamaiso ka molao, boitshoko, katlego le lorato jwa lefatshe, Re leboga botlhokwa jwa Modimo, ka gore botshelo jwa rona re mo yena, Re dirisa Molao-Motheo oo go aga le go tlhabolola lefathse la rona, botlhokwa jwa go direla mo pontshong, tekatekanyo, kgololego, lekanetsa, nnete le go dira la natla, Le go batla tsamaiso ya Modimo, gore batho bakgone go dirisa Molao-Motheo oo mo lefatshe le le rategang.

KGAOLO 1 Dino Tse Di Fitlhetse 1

Lefatshe

Lefatshe la Zimbabwe ke lefatshe la puso ya batho ka batho, ke lefatshe lengwe fela le le ipusang. 2

Botlhokwatlhokwa jwa Molao Motheo

(1) Molao Motheo ke molao o mo tona wa lefatshe la Zimbabwe jalo tse dingwe tse di tlang morago kwa Molao Motheo oo ga di dumelwe.

(2) Taolo ya Molao Motheo go tshwanetse go lemogiwa ke batho botlhe, tlholego, Mmuso, Baeteledipele, Mapalamente, baatlhodi botlhe ba lefatshe le maphata otlhe a goromente. 3

Seabe le tsamaiso ya dino

(1) Lefatshe la Zimbabwe le fithetse mo go hlompheng botlhokwa le tsamaiso ya tse di latelang—

(a)

botlhokwatlhokwa jwa Molao Motheo;

(c)

botlhokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho;

(e)

go lemoga botlhokwa le seabe sa motho;

(b) mmuuso wa molao;

(d) mefutafuta ya setso le ngwao, tumelo le botlhokwa jwa tlholego;

(f)

go lemoga tekatekanyo ya batho botlhe;

(g) tekatekanyo ya bong;

(h) mmuso o siame;

(i)

go lemoga le go hlompha ntwa ya go kgololego;

(2) Tsamaiso ya mmuso o siame, e kopantsang Lefatshe le maphata a ga goromente otlhe a tshwana le

(a)

maphata a se politiki a farologaneng;

(b)

lekgotla la ditlhopho le remeletse mo—

(i) ditshwanelong le tekatekanyo ya maduo a dithopho mo lefatsheng ka bo phara;

(ii) ditlhophong tse di kgololesegileng, lekanetsweng le tse di tshwarang ka nako e beilweng; le

(iii) go lekanetsa maphata a se poliki mo ditlhophong;

(c)

go neilana maatla sentle ka morago ga ditlhopho;

(d) go hlompha ditshwanelo tsa maphata a se politiki;

(e)

go lemoga tsamaiso ya go phatlhalatsa maatla;

(f)

go hlompha batho ba lefatshe la Zimbabwe ka gore taolo ya mmuso e tswa mo go bona;

(g) go tsamaisa ka molao, direla mo pontsong, boikarabelo le seabe sa motho;

(h) go godisa popagano ya batho, kagiso le go itshetletse, tse di akaeretsang mefutafuta ya diteme, setso le tsa tlholego;

(i)

go lemoga ditshwanelo tsa—

(j)

2013

(i) mmala, setso, teme, tlholego le tumelo ya batho;

(ii) batho ba banang le bogole;

(iii) mabutswapele ba ntwa ya kgololego; go a belana tsa tlholego tse di akaretsang lefatshe;

(k) go hlompha di tshwanelo; le

(l) 4

go phatlalatsa maatla le tiro ya goromente.

Folaga ya Setshaba, Pina ya Setshaba, Setlanyo le Sekano sa Morafe

Lefatshe la Zimbabwe le na le Folaga, Pina, Setlanyo le Sekano sa Setshaba, ka se se beilweng mo Rulaganyo ya Ntlha. 5

Di karolo tsa goromente Di karolo tsa goromente wa Zimbabwe ke—

(a)

Goromente wa setshaba;

(c)

Baeteledipele ba di kgaolwana, tse di tshwanang le—

(i) khansele ya toropo go emela le go tsamaisa dino tsa batho ko toropong; le

(ii) khansele ya ko magaeng go emela dino tsa batho ko magaeng;

(b) Khansele ya buntlha jwa lefatshe le toropo; le


Di puo

(1) Dipuo tse di tshwanang le Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda le Xhosa di tshwanetse go lemogiwa le go dirisiwa mo lefatsheng la Zimbabwe.

(2) Molao wa Palamente o tla taola gore ke di fi diteme tse di tla lemogiwa le tse di tshwanetse go kwalwa.

(3) Lefatshe le maphata otlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go—

(a)

Lemoga le go tekanetsa diteme tsotlhe; le

(b) Tsaya pego ya go hlompha thato ya teme mo bathong ba ba amanwang ke di thulaganyo kgotsa melaetsa ya goromente.

(4) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tlhokomela le go godisa tiriso ya diteme tsotlhe mo Zimbabwe go akaretsa puo ya diatla, le go bopa mabaka a ko tlhabolola diteme tsotlhe. 7

Go bapatsa setshaba le temogo ya Molao Motheo Lefatshe le tshwanetse go papatsa setshaba go temoga Molao Motheo ka go—

(a)

Ranola Molao Motheo ka diteme tsotlhe tsa lefatshe le go thuta batho botlhe mo lefatsheng kabo phara;

(b) Batla gore Molao Motheo o rutiwe ko dikolong le gore otla nna dithuto tsa batho ba ba katiswang bokgone mo tsa tshireletso, Badirelapuso; le

(c)

Rotloetsa batho botlhe le maphata otlhe, go akaretsa maphata a setshaba, go kgothatsa papatso le kisto ya Molao Motheo.

KGAOLO 2 Maikaelelo A Setshaba 8

Maikaelelo a go thusa Lefatshe le maphata otlhe a goromente

(1) Maikaelelo a beilweng mo kgaolong ee ke go thusa Lefatshe le maphata otlhe a goromente ka mogopolo wa go bopa le go kgatelela melao e tla thusang go tshimolola, rulaganya le go godisa puso ya batho ka batho go re batho ba itumelele katlego le botshelo jo bontle.

(2) Maikaelelo a kgaolo ee ke go tlhalosa se se batliwang ke Lefatshe mo Molaong Motheo le mengwe melao. 9

Mmuso o siame

(1) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go dirirsa megopolo le molao go aga, go dira ka bonatla, botswerere, boikarebelo, go direla mo potshong, botlhale le kitso etletseng ya madi, mo maphateng otlhe a goromente le maphata a morafe le go bona gore—

(a)

kwa tlhophiwa batho ba ba nnang le bokgoni go bereka ko ofising ya morafe;

(b) morafe wa kgona go temoga, ntsha le go supa ka monwana botlhe ba ba berekang mo ofising ya se politiki le ofisi ya morafe, ba ba tshenyetsang setshaba.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go bona gore maphata otlhe a goromente, thata Balaudi le tse dingwe Dikgotla tse di bopilweng mo Molaong Motheo oo, di tla tlamelwa di dirisiwa gore di kgone go dira ka bo natla, sentle, ka boammaaruri le botswerere. 10

Popagano ya setshaba, kagiso le tshiamo

Lefatshe le mongwe le mongwe motho, baatlhodi le maphata otlhe fela a tshwanetse go godisa popagano, kagiso le tshiamo mo setshabeng. 11

Go godisa botlhokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho

Lefatshe le tshwanetse go ela tlhoko botlhokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho se se beilweng mo Kgaolong ya bonne le go godisa temogo le kgotsofatso. 12

Megopolo ka mafatshe a sele

(1) Megopolo ya Zimbabwe ka mafatshe a sele e tshwanetse go tsamaiswa

jana—

(a)

go godisa le go pabalela se se batliwang ke batho ba Zimbabwe;

(c)

kagiso le mangwe mafatshe a sele; le

(b) go hlompha melao ya mafatshe a sele;

(d) go rarabolola mathata a tlang le mangwe mafatshe a sele ka kagiso.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go godisa sebopego sa Africa mo setsong, tsa madi le se politiki ka go tshwaragana le go popagana le go tsena mo dikgotleng tsa mangwe mafatshe a sele le go emela kagiso le tlhabologo le botho mo mafatsheng a sele. 13

Tlhabologo ya setshaba

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go thusa ka bo nako le go lekanetsa tlhabologo ka kelelelo mo go—

(a)

2013

bona gore mongwe le mongwe motho wa kgona go idirela le go ikemela ka nosi;

(b) godisa temo thuo, papatso, kgwebo, maranyane le sayensi;

(c)

godisa kgwebo le papatso ka go neela baagedi ba Zimbabwe bokgoni; le

(d) lekanetsa tlhabologo ya di kgaolo tsotlhe tsa lefatshe la Zimbabwe, go temogilwe lekanetso ya magae letoropo.

(2) lenaneo la mophato oo ke go batla gore batho botlhe ba fe megopolo ya bone ya tlhabologo le dithulaganyo mo dilong tsotlhe tse di tshwenyang mo maitsholong a bone.

(3) lenaneo la mophato oo ke go batla gore go tlhokomelwe ditshwanelo tsa batho go lebilwe basadi, ka go ba neela mabaka a lekanang mo tlhabologong.

(4) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go bona gore morafe wa tlhabologiwa ke khumo e tlholegang mo kgaolong tsa bone. 14

Go fa bokgoni le go bula mmereko

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go leka go fa dithata, ka go dirisa tshiamo, direla mo pontshong, tekatekanyo le kgatelopele, mo bathong ba ba humanegileng, setlhopha le dikgaolo tsotlhe tsa Zimbabwe.

(2) Malatsi otlhe Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go bula mmereko mo bathong botlhe ba Zimbabwe go lebilwe thata basadi le bonana. 15

Dijo Lefatshe le tshwanetse go—

(a)

lemotsa batho go lema le go boloka dijo tse di lekaneng;

(c)

kgothatsa le go tsholetsa dikotla jo bo lekaneng le jo bo siameng mo bathong ka go fa dithuto le tse dingwe dithulaganyo.

(b) tlhokomela sesigong sa dijo se se lekaneng; le


Setso

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente a tshwanetse lemotsa batho ka go balesega jwa setso le ngwao, gore batho ba Zimbabwe ba nne le seriti, botho le tekatekanyo.

(2) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente le baagedi ba Zimbabwe ba tshwanetse go boloka le go tlhokomela lefa la bone.

(3) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go bona gore batho ba hlompha thata botlhokwa jwa mafelo a tlholego. 17

Tekatekanyo ya bong

(1) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go godisa tekatekanyo ya bong mo setshabeng sa Zimbabwe, ka go—

(a)

pateletse basadi go tsaya kgwetlho e lekanang le ya banna mo setshabeng sa Zimbabwe.

(b) tsaya kgato mmogo le go tirisa molao go bona gore –

(i) bong jwa lekana mo mephatong ya goromente; le

(ii) basadi ba na le palo e lekanang le ya banna mo mephateng yotlhe fela ya goromente se se supiwang ke Molao Motheo oo le melao ya Palamente; le

(c)

Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go pateletsa gore basadi baneelwe di dirisiwa, lefatshe, ka go lekana le banna.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ya go ntsha kgethololo, le go sa lekane kwa bong, ene e le megopolo ya maloba. 18

Go ba balesega mo buntlheng jwa lefatshe jwa tsotlhe jwa Lefatshe

(1) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go ba balesega mo buntlha jwa lefatshe tsotlhe tsa Zimbabwe mo dikgotleng le mephathong yotlhe.

(2) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromebte a tshwanetse go pateletsa gore dikgaolo tsotlhe di nna le di dirisiwa tse di, lekanang go itlhabolola. 19

Bana

(1) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya megopolo ya go tlotla se se batliwang ke

bana.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya megopolo ka tsotlhe tse di kgonegang gore bana ba—

(a)

itumelele le lwapa lwa ga gabo kgotsa batsadi ba bone, kgotsa tlhokomelo ya kwantle kwa le lwapa lwa bone;

(b) nna le felo la tshireletso le dijo jo bo siame, botsogo le go kgathalelwa mo maitshelong a bone;

(c)

tshereletswe mo setlhogong, go tlogelwa ba le nosi kgotsa go sa tshwarwa sentle ka ntha engwe fela; le

(d) nna le thuto le bokgoni.

(3) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go dirisa molao le go tsaya kgato dingwe fela go—

(a)

tshireletsa bana mo go dirisiwa ko tirong ya kgwebo;

(b) bona gore bana a ba berekisiwa kana go dira tiro mo—

(i) mabakeng a bo nyenyane ba bone;

(ii) mafelong a tla senya bana botho, thuto, mmili kgotsa mogopolo kgotsa moya, tumelo kgotsa tlhabologo ya gagwe mo morafeng. 20

Bonana

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go tsaya kgato mo bananeng go tswa mo dingwaga tsa lesome le botlhano go ya go masome a mararo le botlhano gore ba—

(a)

nna le thuto le bokgoni jwa sengwe;

(b) nna le seabe le go ba balesega mo sa politiki, morafeng, marakeng le botshelo bongwe fela;

(c)

ineela mabaka a go batla tiro le go ithotloetsa mo marakeng;

(e)

itshereletsa mo ngwaong e sa seamang, go dirisiwa go sena ma duo a lekaneng le go pateletswa ka ntha engwe fela;

(d) ineela sebaka sa go tlosa bodutu le go etela mafelo a tlosang bodutu; le

(2) Molao wa Palamente o ka neela dithulaganyo e ngwe kana tse di ntsi tsa bonana.

(3) Dikgato le dithulaganyo tse di supiwang mo temana (1) le (2) di tshwanetse go sa somarele ntha engwe le go ratisa motho lefatshe. 21

Bagolo

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe, le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go dirisa molao, tlotla, go hlompha le go tshereletsa bagolo le gore ba nna le seabe mo botshelong jwa dikgaolong tsa bonne.

(2) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go dirisa di dirisiwa tse di tlholegang go—

(a)

thotloetsa bagolo go nna le seabe mo morafeng;

(c)

bula dithulaganyo tse di thusang bagolo go tlhlabolola setshaba ka bokgoni jwa bonne le se ba se kgonang le se ba se batlang; le

(b) neela bagolo ditlamelo, dijo le go kgathalela ba ba tlhokang sengwe;

(d) kopa maphata a kgathalelang batho go nna le maitlamo a go tlhabolola botshelo jwa bagolo. 22

Batho ba banang le bogole

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go lemoga ditshwanelo tsa batho ba banang le bogole mo mmeleng le mo tlhaloganyong, go lebelwe ditshwanelo tsa go hlomphiwa le seriti sa bone;

(2) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go thusa batho ba banang le bogole jwa mmele le tlhaloganyo, ka tsotlhe ba banang le tsone gore ba kgonne go dira ka natla le go tlosa se se ba kgoreletsang;

(3) Kakaretso, Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go—

(a)

bopa dithulaganyo tsa batho ba banang bogole jwa mmele le tlhaloganyo, go lebelwe tiro le bokgonni jwa bonne le go amogela botho ba bone le baemedi ba bone;

(b) lemoga se se batliwang ke batho ba banang bogole ka go farologanang kwa bone, ke kgato ya ntlha mo dithulaganyong tsa tlhabololo ya setshaba;

(c)

rotloetsa go dirisa le go godisa puo e kgonwang ke batho ba banang bogole ka go farologana kwa bone;

(d) kopa maphata a kgathalelang batho go nna le maitlamo a go tlhabolola botshelo jwa batho ba banang bogole.

(e)


Lefatshe le tshwanetse go bona gore mafelo a phithelwa ke morafe otlhe a iphithelwa gape ke batho ba banang le bogole.

Mabutswapele a ntwa ya kgololo (ipuso)

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go hlompha, tlotla le go lemoga otlhe mabutswapele a ntwa ya kgololo (ipuso) ba ba—

(a)

ineetseng go lwa Ntwa ya Kgololo (ipuso);

(c)

tshwerweng, tlhatlhelweng le go emiswa ka mabaka a se politiki ka nako ya ntwa ya kgololo (ipuso).

(b) thusileng ba lwi ba Ntwa ya Kgololo (ipuso); le

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya kgato, mmogo le go dirisa molao go kgathelela le go thotloetsa kgwebo ya mabutswapele a ntwa ya kgololo (ipuso) 24

Tiro le bo tsalano ko tirong

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go tsaya megopolo le kgato ya go neela mongwe le mongwe sebaka sa go bereka ka kgololesego mo se a se batlang, gore batho ba ne le botshelo jo bo siameng.

(2) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go—

(a)

somarela tiro e ntsi mo setshabeng;

(b) tlosa tse di emisang le tse di kgoreletsang batho go bereka le go dira kgwebo e fang madi;

(c)

thotloetsa batho gore ba nne le bokgoni mo tirong e farologaneng;

(d) tsaya kgato ya go babalela le lwapa gore le nne le basadi ba ba berekang. 25

Tshireletso ya le lwapa

Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotlas tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ya go tshireletsa le go godisa le lwapa ka go—

(a)

neela kgathalelo le thuso mo go bo mmaabo, rraabo le botlhe batho ba le lwapa; le

(b) go tshireletsa go tlhoka kutlwisisano ya ba le lwapa. 26

Lenyalo Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ya go ya bona gore—

(a)

ga gona lonyalo lo lo tla diragala fa go sena go dumelana kwa motho le mo ratani wa gagwe;

(b) bana a ba tshwanela go nna mpho mo lo nyalong;

(c) go nna le tekatekanyo ya ditshwanelo le melawana ya baratani mo lonyalong le phatalatso ya lonyalo; le

(d) ka nako ya phatalatso ya lonyalo ka mabaka a lusu le kgaoganyo, dino di teng tsa go tshireletsa bana le balekane. 27

Dithuto

(1) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ya go godisa—

(a) dithuto tse di kgololesegileng le go pateletsa thuto ya ntlha mo baneng;

(b) thuto e golwane le ya godimodimo.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go lekanetsa mabaka a go tsaywa kwa thuto ya basetsana le ya basimane mo mephatong yotlhe fela ya thuto. 28

Lefelo la tshireletso

Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente a tshwanetse go bona gore mongwe le mongwe o na lefelo la tshireletso. 29

Mananeo a botsogo

(1) Lefatshe, maphata otlhe le dikgotla tsotlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go bona gore go na le mananeo a botlhokwa mo botsogong jwa batho mo lefatsheng la Zimbabwe.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go bona gore, ga gona motho o ka ganelwa go neelwa thuso ka bo nako fa go na le tshoganetso ko mafelong a botsogo.

(3) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go thibelela bolwetse jo bo jwelelang, ka go thuta le go dira di thulaganyo tse di lemogisang batho. 30

Go kgathalela le go tirela setshaba Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tshireletsa le go kgathalela batho ba ba tlhokang sengwe.


Thuso ya ka fa molaong

Lefatshe le tshwanetse, go thusa ka go neela moemedi wa molao, mo sekaseko ya go tshenyetsa setshaba le go kutlwisa batho setlhogo, mo bathong ba batlang thuso le ba sa kgoneng go duela baemedi ba molao mo tshekong. 32

Metshameko le ditlamelo tse di tlosang bodutu

Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya tshweetso ya go rotlwetsa batho go iphitisa nako mo metsamokong le mo matlamelong a tlosang bodutu. 33

Tshomarelo ya kitso ya tlholego

Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tshomarela, tshireletsa le go godisa kitso ya tlholego, mmogo le melemo le phologolo le ditlhare tse di tlholegang mo di kgaolong. 34

Go amogela melao ya mafatshe a sele mo lefatsheng

Lefatshe le tshwanetse go amogela melao, megopolo le tumelano ya mafatshe a sele gore e tsenywe mo molaong wa lefatshe. KGAOLO 3 Boagedi 35

Boagedi ba Zimbabwe

(1) Motho o nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka tsholo, ka lesika le kwadiso.

(2) Botlhe baagedi ba Zimbabwe bana le ditshwanelo, tlotlo le go thapiwa pele mo dino le go tsaya seabe le melawana ya boagedi ka go lekana.

(3) Baagedi botlhe ba Zimbabwe ba tshwanetse go neelwa ditshwanelo le go lemogiwa ke molao mo go—

(a)

tshereletswa ke Lefatshe kwa ba tlhokanng teng;

(b) neelwa dipasipoti le tse dingwe di pampiri tsa go tsamaya ko mafatshefatshe;

(c)

neelwa dipampiri tsa botsalo le tse dingwe di pampiri tse di supang gore omang ke Lefatshe.

(4) Baagedi ba Zimbabwe ba nna le seabe ba ba se neelweng ke molao go—

(a)

rata Zimbabwe

(b) lemoga Molao Motheo le go hlompha maphata otlhe a molao le se se taolwang ke molao;

(c)

hlompha folaga ya setshaba le pina ya setshaba;

(d) tshereletsa Zimbabwe le mmuso ka tsotlhe ba banang le tsone.

Boagedi ka matsalo

(1) Motho ke motho wa Zimbabwe fa a tsholelwe mo Zimbabwe le fa a tsholetswe—

(a) ke mmaagwe kgotsa rraagwe a le moagedi wa Zimbabwe;

(b) ke batsadi ba ga mmagwe kgotsaa rragwe ba le baagedi ka matsalo kgotsa losika.

(2) Motho o tsholetswe kwantle kwa Zimbabwe o nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka matsalo fa, batsadi o mongwe wa bone a ne a le moagedi wa Zimbabwe kgotsa—

(a) mo nni hela wa Zimbabwe; kgotsa

(b) motho o berekang kwantle kwa Zimbabwe fa e berekela Lefatshe kana mephato ya mafatshefatshe.

(3) Ngwana o tlholegang, batsadi ba gagwe ba siyo gape ba sa itse batsadi ba gagwe, fa a nna le ngwaga tse di kwatlase kwa lesome le botlhano, o nna moagedi ka matsalo. 37

Boagedi ka losika

(1) Go lebelwe mophato 36(2), motho o tsholetswe kwantle kwa Zimbabwe o nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka losika, fa a tshotswe—

(a)

ke batsadi kana mongwe wa masika a gagwe a bogolobogolo ke baagedi ba Zimbabwe; kana

(b) mongwe wa batsadi ba gagwe a ne a le moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka go kwadisa; ka gore matsholo a kwadilwe ka molao wa di kwadiso tsa matsalo. 38

Boagedi jwa kwadiso

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o na le ngwaga tse tlhano e nyetswe ke moagedi wa Zimbabwe, ka maemo a kgotsofalesang Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go ikwadisa go nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho motho o mo dulo ka molao mo Zimbabwe ka ngwaga tse le some, gape maemo a gagwe a kgotsofalesang Molao wa Palamente o ka ikwadisa go nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe.

(3) Ngwana o seng moagedi wa Zimbabwe, fa e tlhokomelwa ke moagedi wa Zimbabwe gape maemo a gagwe a kgotsofalesang Molao wa Palamente o ka ikwadisa go nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe. 39

Go ntshewa jwa boagedi

(1) Boagedi jwa kwadiso mo Zimbabwe bo ka ntshewa fa—

(a)

motho wa teng o bo tseye ka bo menemene, go supagetsa maka kgotsa o tlosetse dilo tsa nnete; kana

(b) Zimbabwe e ne e le mo ntweng, motho wa teng a ne a thusa ka dilo dingwe, go lwantsha Zimbabwe.

(2) Boagedi ba matsalo bo ka ntshewa fa—

(a)

motho wa teng o bo tseye ka bo menemene, go supagetsa maka kgotsa o tlosetse dilo tsa nnete; kana

(b) motho wa teng o tlhalosiwang ke mo mophato 36 (3), boagedi kgotsa batsadi ba gagwe baitsewa, kwa supagala gore o ne a le moagedi wa lengwe lefatshe.

(3) Boagedi ba Zimbabwe a bo ntshewe ka se tlhalosiwang ke mophatong o o tla nna motho o sena kwa ga gaabo. 40

Go amogela boagedi ka ntlha ya le nyalo kana go phatlalala kwa le nyalo Boagedi jwa Zimbabwe a bo ntshewe ka lo nyalo kana go phatlalala kwa lo nyalo


Lekgotla la boagedi le go ya ko mafatsheng a sele

Palamente e tshwanetse go dira molao o bopang komiti ya Boagedi le Go ya ko mafatheng a sele e na le modulastilo le batho ba babedi ba ba tlhophilweng ke Tautona, go tsaya kgato ya go—

(a)

neela le go ntsha boagedi jwa kwadiso;

(b) dumela batho ba sele go nna le go bereka mo Zimbabwe le go baya melawana le mabaka a go nna le go bereka; le

(c) 42

dira tiro ba ba ineetsweng kgotsa ba ba ipateletswang ke Molao wa Palamente.

Maatla a Palamente a akanyetsa boagedi

Molao wa Palamente o ka dira dino, tse di tsamayelanang le Kgaolo ya lesome le boraro ka go—

(a)

tsaya boagedi jwa Zimbabwe ka go ikwadisa go tsamaiswa jang

(b) ntsha boagedi jwa Zimbabwe ka lorato lwa motho;

(c)

tsamaisa go ntshewa jwa boagedi jwa Zimbabwe ka go ikwadisa;

(e)

gana boagedi jwa mafatshe a mabedi ka nthata ya losika kana ikwadiso; le

(f)

dirisa melawana ya Kgaolo ee.

(d) go amogela boagedi jwa Zimbabwe;

43 Tswelediso le pusetso ya boagedi jwa pele

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho motho otla tswelela a nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka mo rago kwa go kwalwa kwa molao omusha.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tsholetswe mo Zimbabwe pele ka go kwala kwa molao ba nna moagedi wa Zimbabwe ka matsalo fa gore—

(a)

mongwe kana botlhe batsadi ba gagwe ba ne ba le baagedi ba mangwe mafatshe a kgotsa Mafatshe a Africa Borwa ( Southern African Development Community) e botswe ke molao o dirilwe ko toropong ya Windhoek ko lefatsheng la Namibia ka le tsatsi la lesome le bosupa mo kgweding ya Phatwe ka ngwaga wa 1992; le

(b) kana a le mo nni hela wa Zimbabwe ka letsatsi la go kwalwa kwa molao oo.

KGAOLO 4 Ditshwanelo Tsa Batho KAROLO 1 Tiriso Le Tlhaloso Ya Kgaolo 4 44

Tiro ya go hlompha botlhokwa jwa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho

Lefatshe, batho botlhe, mmogo le baatlhodi botlhe ba lefatshe, le maphata otlhe a goromente a tshwanetse go hlompha, pabalela, godisa le go kgotsofatsa ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho e beilweng mo Kgaolong ya Bonne. 45

Tiriso ya Kgaolo ya Bonne

(1) Kgaolo e e patika Lefatshe, bookamedi botlhe, Molao Motheo, maphata otlhe a boatlhodi le dikgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente.

(2) Kgaolo e e patika batho botlhe ba tlholego le basekisi go fitlha, go lebelwe ditshwanelo le kgololego le tiro ya go dirisa molao oo mo go bonne.

(3) Basekisi le batho ba tlholego ba neelwa ditshwanelo le kgololego e beilweng mo Kgaolong ee go fitlha ditshwanelo le kgololego e e oketswa ka go tlhamalala. 46

Tlhaloso ya Kgaolo 4

(1) Fa go tlhalosiwa Kgaolo ee, dikgotla, tlotlo ya motho, komiti—

(a)

di tshwanetse go neela ditshwanelo le kgololego yotlhe e beilweng mo Kgaolo ee;

(b) di tshwanetse go godisa seabe le tsamaiso e batliwang ke puso ya batho ka batho go temogiwa molao motheo wa kgololesego, ditsamaiso tsa molao, seriti sa motho, tekatekanyo le kgololego, go lebelwe thata, seabe le tsamaiso e beilweng mo mophatong 3;

(c)

di tshwanetse go temoga molao wa mafatshefatshe le melawana le maraka ka gore Zimbabwe e moteng;

(d) di tshwanetse go akanyetsa dino tsotlhe tse di mo Molaong Motheo oo, go lebelwe ditsamasio le maikaelelo a beilweng mo Kgaolo ya Bobedi; le

(e)

go dirisa molao wa mafatshefatshe fa go tshwanetse go dirisa one;

(2) Fa go tlhalosiwa tiriso ya molao motheo, le go bopa mengwe melao le melao ya setso, dikgotla tsotlhe, tlotlo ya motho, komiti e tshwanetse go godisa le go tlhokomela maitlamo le maikaelelo a Kgaolo ee. 47

Kgaolo ya Bonne a ya tshwanela go kgoreletsa dingwe ditshwanelo

Kgaolo ee a ya tshwanela go kgoreletsa dingwe ditshwanelo le kgololego tse di ka temogiwang kgotsa tse di neelwang ke molao, go feta bogolo e sa fapaana le Molao Motheo.

KAROLO 2 Botlhokwa Jwa Ditshwanelo Le Kgololego 48

Di tshwanelo tsa botshelo

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya botshelo.

(2) Molao o ka dumela katlholo ya kotlhao ya lusu fa gore motho o na le molato wa go bolaya motho ka maemo a maswafatsang, le—

(a)

molao o ka dumela gore kgotla e bonang ka teng go gana go sa patike kotlhao;

(b) kotlhao e ka dirwa fa katlholo ya bofelo e tswa mo kgotlheng e nna le botswerere;

(c) kotlhao a ya patikwa mo mothong—

(i) o dirileng molato ngwaga tsa gagwe di le kwatlase jwa masome ama bedi le motso; kgotsa

(ii) ngwaga tsa gagwe di kwa godimo jwa masome ama supa;

(d) kotlhao a ya patikwa mo basading; le

(e) motho o katlhotsweng o ka batla go ntshewa kana go fetolwa kwa kotlhao ke Tautona.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go pabalela bana ba ba ntse ba le mo mmaleng, gape Molao o o tshwanetse go baya mabaka a ko tlosa boimana ka tiriso ya molao oo. 49

Ditshwanelo tsa kgololesego ya motho

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o na le tshwanelo ya go kgololesega, mmogo le tshwanelo ya—

(a) go sa tlhatlhelwa go sena tsheko; le

(b) go neelwa kgololesego e sentle kana ka go lekanetsa dilo.

(2) go sa tlhatlhelwa ka mabaka a go sa kgoni go kgotsofatsa melawana ya mmereko. 50

Ditshwanelo tsa go tshwarwa le go tlhatlhelwa

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tsherweng—

(a) o tshwanetse go tsibisiwa ka lo baka lwa go tshwarwa, molato wa gagwe;

(b) o tshwanetse go dumelwa, go sena go diega –

(i) go bua le mo lekane, tsala, mongwe wa masika a gagwe, moemedi wa gagwe wa molao, kana mongwe fela motho a mo ratang, ke molato wa Lefatshe go duela; le

(ii) go bua le moemedi wa molao wa gagwe kana ngaka ya gagwe, jalo o tla iduelela; o tshwanetse go lemogiswa kabo nako;

(c) o thwanetse go tshwara sa motho ka maitseo ka gore o nna le seriti sa lefa.

(d) o tshwanetse go ntshewa go sena go somarela ntlha engwe kana ka mabaka mangwe a tlotla, a ntse a emetse katlholo kana tsheko ya gagwe, fa go nna le sengwe se se kgotsofatsang o ka tswelela a ntse a tlhatlhetswe; le

(e) o tshwanetse go dumelwa go botsolotsa molao o dirisitsweng go mo tshwara, ko kgotlheng Gape o tshwanetse go togelwa go ka bo nako, fa go tshwara kwa gagwe go siyo mo molaong

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tshwereng kana go tlhatlhelwa—

(a)

ka mabaka o go mo emisa mo kgotleng hela; kana

(b) ka nthatha ya molato; le o sa ntshewa o tshwanetse go ya ko kgotlheng ka bo nako gape a kwa tshwanele go feta ma awara a le masome a manne le borobabobedi pele kwa go tshwarwa kana go tlhatlhelwa, go ntse jalo nako ya ema ka Matlhatso,Tshipi, kgotsa ka malatsi a itapoloso.

(3) Mongwe le mongwe motho o sa tsenang ko kgotlheng go feta bo leele jwa di awara a le masome a manne le borobabobedi se se beilweng mo temana (2) o tshwanetse go ntshewa go ne ka bonako fa go sena taulo ya kgotla e na le botswerere gore a tlhatlhelwe lobaka jo bo leele.

(4) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tshwereng kana go tlhatlhelwa ka nthata ya molato o na le tshwanelo ya—

(a)

go didimala;

(b) go tsibisiwa ka bonako—

(i) go didimala; le

(ii) mabaka a go bua kana go didimala;

(c)

go sa pateletswa go dumela kana ko amogela; le

(d) go tsibisiwa mabaka a go tswelela a ntse a tlhatlhetswe kana go ntshewa, fa e tsena ko kgotlheng jwa ntlha

(5) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tlhatlhelweng, mmogo le mo kgolegelwa o ntse a le mo toronkong, o nna le tshwanelo ya—

(a)

go tsibisiwa mabaka a ntse a tlhatlhetswe;

(b) go tsibisiwa ka bo nako, gore o nna le tshwanelo ya go bua le moemedi wa molao wa gagwe a le nosi, gape o tla iduelela;

(c)

go bua, kana go etelwa ke –

(i) mo lekane wa gagwe kana tsala ya gagwe;

(ii) mongwe wa masika a gagwe;

(iii) mongwe a mo batlang wa tumelo ya gagwe;

(iv) moemedi wa molao a mo batlang; principles

(v) ngaka ya gagwe a ibatlang; le

(vi) mongwe le mongwe fela fa a dumelwa ke baeteledipele ba kgologelo kana lefelo la tlhatlhelo;

(d) maemo a tlhatlhelo gore a siame mo siriteng sa motho, mmogo le go neelwa sebaka sa go itshidisa mmele, ke molato wa Lefatshe, go bona gore go nna le kamelo le le siame, toilete, botsogo jo bo siame, dijo jo bo lekaneng, di buka tsa go bala tse di siameng le kalafi ya ngaka; le

(e)

go batla botsoloso ko kgotleng a le esi, gore molao wareng ka tlhatlhelo ya gagwe, fa gore a ga go yo mo molaong o tshwanetse go ntshewa ka bo nako.

(6) mongwe le mongwe motho o tlhatlhelweng go emetse katlholo gape a sena go sekisiwa ka lobaka jo bo siameng o tshwanetse go ntshewa mo tlhatlhelong ka mabaka a—

(a)

go ya go sekiswa;

(c)

gore a baa tshwanela go dira mongwe molato pele ka nako ya tsheko.

(b) go kgoreletsa tshupo e tla neelwang ko tshegong;

(7) Fa go solofelwa gore motho o tlhatlhetswe go se mo molaong kgotsa go sa itsiwe gore o tlhatlhetswe kae, mongwe le mongwe motho o kgona go ya ko Kgotlheng e Kgolo go tsaya tshwetso—

(a)

ya go kopa gore motho o tlhatlhetswe a ntshewe, kgotsa go ya ko kgotlheng go botsa mabaka a go tlhatlhelwa jwa gagwe, kgotsa go kopa go itse gore motho o tlhatlhetswe o ko kae kgotsa

(b) ya go bontsa gore tlhatlhelo ya motho a iyo mo molaong jalo o tshwanetse go ntshewa ka bonako; gape Kgotla-Kgolo e tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ka maemo aa.

(8) Fa gore go tshwarwa kgotsa go tlhatlhelwa a go tsamaelane le mophato oo, kgotsa maemo a tshawaraganang le mophato oo, go raya gore tlhatlhelo ya teng a iyo mo molaong.

(9) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tshwereng kgotsa go tlhatlhelwa kwantle kwa molao o tshwanetse go duelwa ke motho o mo tshwaresileng kgotsa go tlhatlhelwa, me molao o kgona go pabalela bangwe batho ba ba supiwang ke mophato oo, ba ba tshwanang le—

(a)

moatlhodi o emetse mo boatlhodi ka boamaruri le tshepamo;

(b) mongwe le mongwe mo direlapuso o eme ka boammaaruri le tshepamo le go dira ka bomo kana tlhaelo jwa bokgoni. 51

Ditshwanelo tsa seriti sa motho

Mongwe le mongwe motho a na le seriti sa tlholego mo botshelong jwa gagwe a ikemetse ka nosi kana mo morafeng, jalo o nna le tshwanelo ya gore seriti sa gagwe go hlomphiwa le go pabalelwa. 52

Ditshwanelo tsa tshireletso ya motho a ikemetse ka nosi

Mongwe le mongwe o nna le tshwanelo mo mmeleng le tiriso ya tlhaloganyo ka boammaaruri, ka mmogo le ditshwanelo tsa—

(a)

kgololego mo dikgoka tsotlhe mo dilo tsa morafe kgotsa mo go ene a le nosi;

(b) kitso ya dino tsotlhe mo Molaong Motheo oo, le go tsaya tshwetso mabapi le go dirwa sesha sa molao;

(c)


go sa mmusiwa go dira lekeletso mo go direng le kalafi kgotsa sayensi, kana go ntsha kgotsa go dirisa sengwe sa mmele wa gagwe, go sena tumalano pele.

Kgololego mo go sa tlhokofatsewa kgotsa go tlhogofatsewa, nyenyafatswa kana tlontlololwa kana kotlhatswa

Ga gona motho o ka mmusiwa go tlhokofatsewa kgotsa go dirwa setlhogo, nyenyafatsewa kana go tlontlololwa kana go kotlhatswa. 54

Kgololego mo go sa kgobetsewa Ga gona motho o tshwaneste go mmusiwa go kgobegwa.


Kgololego mo go sa dirisiwa tiro ka go pateletsewa Ga gona motho o tshwaneste go dira tiro ka go pateletsewa.

Tekatekano le go kgethelola ope

(1) Batho botlhe ba a lekana mo molaong gape ba nna le tshwanelo ya go pabalesegwa kana go tlhola maduo a lekanang mo molaong;

(2) Basadi le banna ban a le tshwanelo ya go tshwarwa ka go lekana, mmogo le tshwanelo ya go neelwa mabaka a lekanang mo se politiki, ikhonomi, setso le go kgathalelwa mo go tsothle fela.

(3) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya go tshwarwa go sena kgethololo ka go dirisa boagedi jwa gagwe, morafe, mmala, teme, lefelo la matsalo, masika, letso, maemo, tumelo, go sokamela se tlhopha sa politiki, mogopolo, twaelo, setso, bong, go nyala, bogolo jwa motho, go imana, bogole kgotsa maemo a motho mo ikhonomi kana mo morafeng, ba tsholetswa mo kana kwantle kwa lonyalo.

(4) Motho o ka tshwarwa ka kgethololo ka ntatha ya temana (3) fa—

(a)

ba mmusiwa gone jaana kgotsa ka tsela nngwe ka ntatha ya maemo, mabaka kana bogole fa gore gona le batho ba ba sena go mmusiwa.

(b) bangwe batho ba neelwa maemo a botoka go feta yene.

(5) Kgethololo e mo mo temana (3) a wa seama fa gore e supa kgethololo enna lekanetso, mabaka le tsamaiso ya molao mo pusong ya batho ka batho e emetseng mo bonneteng, tsamaiso ya molao, seriti sa motho, tekatekanyo le kgololego.

(6) Lefathse le tshwanetse go dirisa molao le go tsaya kgato go godisa tswelelo pele ya tekatekanyo le pabalelo kgotsa tokafatsa kgotsa maemo a batho ba ba sa neelwang maemo a bo toka ka ntatha ya kgethololo e sa seamang, le—

(a)

fa go ntse jalo go tshwanenetse go tsaywa kgato ya go bakanya mabaka aa ka nnete;

(b) ga gona kgato e ka reywa a ya nna sentle ka ntatha ya temana (3) 57

Ditshwanelo tsa motho a le nosi

Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le ditshwanelo tsa gagwe a le esi, mmogo le ditshwanelo tsa gore a kwa tshwanela go—

(a)

tsena mo le lwapeng, dikagong kgotsa mo lefatsheng la gagwe;

(c)

phamola dilo tsa gagwe;

(e)

upolola maemo a botsogo jwa gagwe;

(b) phuruphutsa ene, lelwapa, kago kgotsa lefatshe la gagwe;

(d) kgoreletsa ditlhaeletsano tsa gagwe;


Kgololego ya kgobokano le kgolagano

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o na le ditshwanelo tsa kgobokano le kgolagano, gape o nna le tshwanelo ya go sa kgobokang kgotsa kgolagana le ba bangwe.

(2) Ga gona motho o ka patikwa go nna le loka la setlhopha kgotsa go tsena mo phuthegong kana kokoano. 59

Kgololego ya go supegetsa le go kopa ka mokwalo wa semmuso

Mongwe le mongwe motho a nna le ditshwanelo tsa go supegetsa le go baya kopo ka mokwalo wa semmuso, jaaka ditshwanelo tse di tshwanetse go dirwa ka kagiso. 60

Kgololego ya letswalo

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le ditshwanelo ya letswalo, mmogo le—

(a)

2013

kgololego mo kakanyo, mogopolo, tshwetso, tumelo; le

(b) kgololego ya go dira sengwe ka twaelo le go ikatisa le go supa maikutlo, mogopolo, tumelo, a le mo morafeng kana a ikemetse ka nosi le fa le esi kana a na le ba bangwe.

(2) Ga gona motho o ka patikwa go tsaya maikano a sa tsamaelane le tumelo ya gagwe kgotsa go tsaya maikano ka mokgwa o sa tsamaelane le tumelo ya gagwe.

(3) Batsadi le ba tlhokomeli ba bana ba ba nyenyane ba nna le ditshwanelo tsa go pateletsa, ka fa tshwanelo ya tumelo ya bone, maitshwaro le tumelo mo go godiseng bana, fa gore a ba kgoreletsa ditshwanelo tsa bana tse di beilweng mo Molaong Motheo oo, mmogo le ditshwanelo tsa thuto, botsogo, pabalelo le kgathelelo.

(4) Engwe le engwe kgaolo ya setshaba e tshwanetse go bula di kgotlha tsa go kaela di tumelo, di kgotlha tsee di kgona go neelwa ditlhwatlhwa tse di kwa tlase kgotsa madi ke Lefatshe. 61

Kgololego ya go supa maikutlo le kgololego ya go bega le go amogela dikgang, kitso le melaetsa

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le kgololego ya go supa maikutlo, mmogo

le—

(a)

kgololego ya go senka, amogela le go bua mogopolo le kitso engwe;

(c)

kgololego ga dithuto.

(b) kgololego ya go supa bodiragatsi le go senka tsa sayensi le bokgoni; le

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le kgololego ya beya kana go amogela dikgang, mmogo le pabalelo ya sephiri sa metswedi ya kitso tsa ba bega dikgang.

(3) Kgaso le go berekisa motlakase go tsamaisa dikgang, kitso le melaetsa go nna le kgololego ya go botswa, go mmusiwa fela ka tsamaiso ya teseletso ke Lefatshe ga—

(a)

konokono ya go laola makhubu a moya le mangwe ma kanamisa matshwawo; le

(b) a ikemeste kanosi go se na taulo ya Lefatshe kgotsa sepolitiki kgotsa ga kgwebo moteng.

(4) Di dirisiwa tsotlhe tsa Lefatshe tsa go tsamaisa dikgang, kitso le melaetsa di tshwanetse go—

(a)

kgololega go laola ka nosi go dira di paakanyo tsa mafoko a kgaswang kgotsa tse di tlhaeletswang;

(b) sa sekemala ntlheng epe; le

(c)

kgona go tekateketsa sebaka sa go tshupegetsa fapaagana jwa pono le kopanyo ya megopolo.

(5) Kgololego ya go supa maikutlo le kgololego ya go bega dikgang, kitso le melaetsa, a kwa tshwanela go—

(a)

thotloetsa dikgoka;

(c)

sola dikgobalo mo seriting sa motho;

(b) bualelela letlhoo kgotsa go ila thata;

(d) roba tumalano ya ditshwanelo tsa motho a le esi ka go sola.

Tshwanelo ya go tlhola kitso

(1) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi wa Zimbabwe kgotsa mo nni ga sennelaruri , mmogo le baatlhodi le go bega le go amogela dikgang, kitso le melaetsa mo Zimbabwe, go nna le ditshwanelo tsa go tlhola kisto e tshwere ke Lefatshe kgotsa di kgotla tsotlhe tsa goromente, fa gore kitso ya teng e nna le kgatlhego mo boekarabelong mo setshabeng.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho, mmogo le go bega le go amogela dikgang, kitso le melaetsa mo Zimbabwe, go nna le di tshwanelo tsa go tlhola engwe le engwe kisto e tshwerwe ke mongwe le mongwe motho, mmogo ke Lefatshe, fa gore kitso ya teng ya dirisiwa kana pabalela engwe tshwanelo.

(3) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya go paakanya kitso, kgotsa go phimola kitso e se nnete, ya phosego kgotsa e ntsha tseleng, e tshwere ke Lefatshe kgotsa lengwe fela le kgotla la goromente, e tsamaelana le motho wa teng.

(4) Molao o tshwanetse go dumelwa go taola di tshwanelo ee, mme o ka kganela go tlhola kitso ka kgatlhego ya sesireletsi, tshereletso ya setshaba, kgotsa sephiri sa tiro ya motho, fa gore kganelo ya teng e nna le go lekanetsa, mabaka a botlhokwa, pabalesego le tsamaiso ya molao mo pusong ya batho ka batho e eme nokeng mo bonneting, ditsamaiso tsa molao, seriti sa motho, tekatekanyo le kgololego. 63

Diteme le setso Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le ditshwanelo ya—

(a)

go tlhopha teme a ibatlang go dirisa ; le

(b) go tlhopha setso a se batlang mo botshelong; mme a gona motho o ka dirisa ditshwanelo tse ka tsela e sa tsamaelane le Kgaolo ee. 64

Kgololego ya tiro, kgwebo le go iphithisa-nako

Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya go tlhopha le go dira engwe le engwe tiro, kgwebo, kgotsa go iphithisanako, mme go dira tiro, kgwebo, kgotsa go iphithisanako go ka taolwa ke molao. 65

Ditshwanelo tsa badiri

(1) Mongwe le mongwe o nna le ditshwanelo tsa lekanyo le ikatiso ya tiro e pabalesegileng le selekanyo sa boleng le go duelwa tuelo e lekanetseng le mabaka a siame.

(2) Kwantle ga ba diredi ba tshereletso, mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le ditshwanelo tsa go bopa le go tsena mo lekgotleng la badiri le mo bereki kgotsa mo hiri go tlhopha kgotla a ibatlang.

(3) Kwantle ga ba diredi ba tshereletso, mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le ditshwanelo tsa go nna le seabe mo go ngongorega ka tiro, mmogo le ditshwanelo tsa ngalo ditiro, ngongorego ya nna fa fatshe, go ntsha bodiredi jwa bonne le tsaya engwe tsela e tshwana le ena, mme molao o ka kganela go dira ditshwanelo tsee gore bodiredi jo botlhokwa bo tsamaye sentle.

(4) Mongwe le mongwe modiri o tshwanetse go kgotsofala, tekatekanetso le tsamaiso ya molao mo mabakeng a tiro.

(5) Kwantle ga ba diredi ba tshereletso, mongwe le mongwe modiredi, mohiri, lekgotla la badiri, le lekgotla la modiredi le mohiri o nna le ditshwanelo tsa go—

(a)

tsena mo tumalano ya sesolo e kopanetsweng;

(b) kgobogana; le

(c)

bopa le go tsena mo lekokong la maphata le makgotla a badiri a tshwanang le aa.

(6) Basadi le banna ba le ditshwanelo tsa go duelwa ka go lekana.

(7) Badiri ba basadi ba le ditshwanelo tsa go duelwa madi otlhe mokgweding tse tharo a ntse a le mo malatsing a go ikhutsa ka mabaka a go tshola. 66

Kgololego ya go tsamaya le bonno

(1) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi wa Zimbabwe o nna le—

(a)

tshwanelo ya go tsena mo Zimbabwe;

(c)

tshwanelo ya pasipoti kgotsa di pampiri tsa tsamaiso

(b) tshireletsego tsa go ntshewa mo Zimbabwe; le

(2) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi wa Zimbabwe le mongwe le mongwe o mo Zimbabwe ka molao o nna le tshwanelo ya go—

(a)

tsamaya ka kgololesego mo Zimbabwe;

(c)

tswa mo Zimbabwe.

(b) nna gongwe le gongwe mo dikgaolong tsa Zimbabwe; le


Ditshwanelo tsa sepolitiki

(1) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi wa Zimbabwe o nna le tshwanelo ya –

(a)

di tlhopho tse di kgololegileng, lekaneng le tse di tla kanako e beilweng, tsa ba bereki ba ofisi ya setshaba e botswileng mo Molaong Motheo kgotsa engwe le engwe melao; le

(b) go itlhophela tsa sepolitiki ka kgololego.

(2) Ka go mmusiwa ke Molao Motheo, mongwe le mongwe moagedi wa Zimbabwe o nna le tshwanelo—

(a)

ya go bopa, go tsena le go nna le seabe mo le kgotleng la sepolitiki kgotsa lephata a itlhopileng;

(b) go dira mananeo a go gapa maikutlo ka kgololesego le ka kagiso mo lekgotleng la sepolitiki kgotsa thulaganyo;

(c)

go nna le seabe mo di thulaganyong tsa sepolitiki;

(d) go nna le seabe, a ikemetse ka nosi kgotsa go kopantswe le ba bangwe, mo kokoanong kgotsa bokopanong kgotsa ka tsela engwe fela, mo di thulaganyong ka kagiso go dira mananeo a go gapa maikutlo, go ngongorega kgotsa go tumalana le megopolo ya Mmuso kgotsa enwe le lengwe le lengwe lekgotla la sepolitiki kgotsa engwe fela thulaganyo.

(3) Ka go mmusiwa ke Molao Motheo, mongwe le mongwe moagedi o nna le ngwaga tse lesome le borobabobedi kgotsa go feta o nna le tshwanelo—

(a)

ya tlhopha mo di tlhophong le mo di tlhopong tsa putso tsotlhe ka tlhaloso ya Molao Motheo kgotsa mongwe molao, ka sephiri; le

(b) go nna ntlhopheng go tsena mo ofising ya setshaba gape f aka tlhopiwa o tshwanetse go nna motshwari wa ofising yone.

(4) Ka mabaka a go godisa gore go nne makgotla a ntsi a sepolitiki, Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go bona gore makgotla a sepolitiki a neelwa madi.

Tswanelo ya botsamaisi ka molao

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya maitshwaro botsamaisi jo bo mo molaong, dirang ka bo nako, berekang sentle, nnang mabaka a botlhokwa, bontlha jwa sengwe, sa somarelang ope le kotlhe go fa bosupi go rurifatsa sengwe le go tlhamalatsa dilo.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo, kgololego, a se batlang kgotsa go le ka fa molaong a go solofetsang jo bo dirilwe mo go maswe ke maitshwaro a botsamaisi o nna le tshwanelo ya go neelwa ka bo nako ka mokwalo mabaka a maitshwara a teng.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go neela di tshwanelo, le go tsaya kgato ya go—

(a)

neela maatla le kgotla go lebelela maitshwaro a botsamaisi kgotsa, fa gore kwa tlhokagala, kgotla e ikemesi ka nosi e ka tsaya tshwetso;

(b) pateletsa tiro mo Lefatsheng go neela di tshwanelo tse di mo mophathonyana wa ntlha le wa ba bobedi; le

(c)

godisa botsaimisi gop bereka sentle.

69 Ditshwanelo tsa go reetsiwa jo bo lekaneng

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o solofelwang gore o nna le molato o nna le tshwanelo ya go sekisiwa mo kgotleng ya setshaba gape ka go lekana ka nako e siameng pele a ya ko kgotleng e ikemesi ka nosi.

(2) Fa kwa bonagala gore di tshwanelo le maikarabelo a setshaba, mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo e lekaneng, ka bofefo, le go reetsiwa mo morafeng ka nako e siameng pele a yak o kgotleng e ikemesi ka nosi, kana engwe fela tsela e bopilweng ke molao.

(3) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya go ya ko dikgotleng, kgotsa engwe fela tsela e bopilweng ke molao go tharabolola engwe le engwe ngangisano.

(4) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo, ka tlhwatlhwa ya gagwe, go tlhopha le emelwa ke motho wa molao mo engwe le engwe kgotla kana gongwe fela. 70

Ditshwanelo tsa batho ba ba latofetsweng

(1) Mongwe le moingwe motho o latofetsweng o nna le ditshwanelo tse di latelang—

(a)

o akanyetswa a se na molato go fitlhelela a rurifatsiwa gore o nna le molato;

(b) go itsiwa ka bo nako molato wa gagwe, ntlha e lekaneng gore a letle go tharabolola;

(c)

go neelwa sebaka le ditlamelo tse di kgotsofatsang go baakanya se sireletso;

(d) go tlhopha mmueledi le, ka tlhwatlhwa ya gagwe, go mo emela mo kgotleng

(e)

(f)

go emelwa ke mmueledi a ineetsweng ke Lefatshe jalo ke tlhwatlhwa ya Lefatshe, fa go le botlhokwa kgobelelo e tla diragala; go itsiwa ka bo nako ditshwanelo tse di neetsweng mo temana (d) le (e);

(g) gore a ba a le teng ka nako ya go sekiswa;

(h) go tsaya kgwetlho le go botsolotsa bosupi;

(i)

go nna a didimetse le go sa supi kgotsa go patikwa go ntsha bosupi jo bo mo tshwarisang

(j)

gore tsheko e sekisiwang e tlhalosewe ka puo a ikutlwang;

(k) go sa bonwa molato ka kgato kgotsa sengwe se se ntshitsweng se ne se se molato ka nako se se diragala;

(l)

go sa bonwa molato ka kgato kgotsa sengwe se se ntshitsweng gore a se molato gompieno;

(m) go sa sekisiwa ka molato ka kgato kgotsa sengwe se se ntshitsweng e ne ya tlosiwa kgotsa togelwa kana go bonwa molato ka maemo;

(n) go katlholwa ka bo tlhofotlhofo mo kotlhao e laetsweng fa gore kotlhao e laetsweng ka mo molatong go nna le phetogo go tswa mo nako ya go dira molato go ya mo nako ya go katlhola;

(2) Fa gore mo mophatong oo kwa tlhokagala gore kitso e neelwe motho—

(a)

kitso ya teng e tshwanetse go neelwa ka pou a ikutlwang; le

(b) fa gore motho ga kgoni go bala kana go kwalo, mokwalo o o nna le kitso ya teng o o tshwaneste go tlhalosiwa ka mokgwa a ikutlwang.

(3) Fa go sekisiwa molato wa borukutlhi, bosupi jo bo teng bo tserwe ka maemo a kgoroletsang dino tsa Kgaolo ee bo tshwanetse go ntshewa fa gore bo tla dira gore motho a dumele bosupi jwa teng fa go ntse jalo tshekiso ya teng a ya lekana kgotsa bokoa jwa bo tsamaisi ka molao kgotsa se se solofetswang ke setshaba.

(4) Mongwe le mongwe motho o sekilweng ka molato o nna le tshwanelo, go duela madi a lekaneng e laetsweng ke molao, go neela se tshwantsho sa pampiri e supang tsheko ka nako e siame ka morago ga katlholo ya lekgotla.

(5) Mongwe le mongwe motho o sekilweng ka molato gape a bonwa molato o nna le tshwanelo, go mmusiwa ka mabaka a kganetsweng ka go laelwa ke molao, go—

(a)

batla molato wa gagwe o lemogiwe ke lekgotla le le gololwane; kgotsa

(b) botsolotsa mo kgotleng e golwane ka go bonwa molato le go katlholwa 71

Ditshwanelo tsa kago kgotsa lefatshe la mongwe

(1) Mo mophatong oo— “tuelo ya pensene” go raya gore madi a bogodi, madi a itshetso ka ngwaga, madi a ditebogo kgotsa tuelo a tshwana le madi a itshetso e duelwang—

(a)

mo go mongwe le mongwe motho a tswa mo Madeng a Kgomotso a Lefatshe kgotsa Kompone;

(b) go hlompha bodiredi jwa motho ke mohiri;

(c)

go hlompha go lwala kgotsa kgobalo ya motho; kgotsa

(d) go hlompha bogodi kgotsa go lwala kgotsa a fela mabaka; gape go akaretsa go dira o ko tshwanang le pensene, madi a itshetso ka ngwaga, madi a a ditebogo kgotsa madi aa itshetso fela le go busa madi a koleketsiweng go duela pensene, madi a tshetso ka ngwaga, madi a ditebogo le madi a itshetso fela; “kago kgotsa lefatshe la mongwe” go raya sengwe le sengwe se tlhalosiwang ka maemo le engwe tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego mo kagong kgotsa lefatshe.

(2) Mmusiwa ke mophato 72, mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo, mo engwe le engwe karolo ya Zimbabwe, go tsaya, go nna le sengwe, go nna mo lefelong, go tshara, go dirisa, go fudusa, go sa tlhomamisiwa, go hirisetsa kgotsa go ipaakanya engwe le engwe kago kgotsa lefatshe, a le nosi kgotsa bokopano le ba bangwe.

(3) Mmusiwa ke mophato oo le mophato 72, ga gona motho o tla patelesegwa go tseelwa kago ya gagwe kgotsa lefatshe la gagwe fa gore mabaka a latelang a kgotsofatswa—

(a)

go tseelwa kwa tlhalosiwa ke molao;

(b) fa gore go tseelwa go botlhokwa ka mabaka a a latelang—

(i) ka kgatlego ya tshereletso, go pabalela setshaba, go tlhomaganya setshaba, go tshiamisa setshaba, botsogo jwa setshaba kgotsa toropo le go dira lenaneo la Lefatshe; kgotsa

(ii) go tlhomamisa tlhabololo kgotsa go dirisa kago kana lefatshe kgotsa dingwe fela dilo go nna morokgotso mo morafeng;

(c)

molao o batla tsela ya go nna le bolaodi—

(i) neela kitsiso e botlhokwa ka maikaelelo a go tsaya kago kgotsa lefatshe mo go mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le kgatlhego kgotsa tshwanelo mo kagong kgotsa mo lefatsheng o ka kgoreletswa ke go tsaywa kwa kago kgotsa lefatshe;

(ii) go duela ka go lekana le go phimola dikeledi se se kgotsofatsang se se tserweng pele kwa go tsaya kago kgotsa lefatshe kana ka nako e siame morago kwa go tsaya sengwe; le

(iii) fa gore tsela ya go tsaya sengwe ya kgaiswa, go ya ko kgotleng e nna le bokgoni pele jwa go tsaya kago kgotsa lefatshe, kgotsa ka malatsi a sa feteng masome a mararo morago kwa go tsaya sengwe, tiriso ya tlhomaganyo e rurifatsang tsela ya go tsaya sengwe;

(d) molao o fa tetla mo go mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le kago e tserweng kgotsa lefatshe le le tserweng go yak o kgotleng e nna le bokgoni go buisiwa kwa kago kgotsa lefatshe ka bo nako fa gore lekgotla le sena go rurifatsa tsela ya go tsaya sengwe; le

(e)

molao o fa tetla mongwe le mongwe o nna sesagagwe go ya ko kgotleng e nna le bokgoni ka maikaelelo a go—

(i) nna teng, maemo le botlhokwa jwa kgatlhego mo kagong kgotsa mo lefatsheng a amiwang;

(ii) bona gore molao wareng ka go rontshwa; le

(iii) bona selekanyo sa go phimolwa dikeledi e mo tetleng ya gagwe;

Le go yak o kgotleng go tsaya tlhomamiso e kaelang go phimolwa dikeledi ka bo nako.

(4) Fa gore go na le motho o nna tshwanelo ka molao kgotsa phosagalo mo tuelong morokgotso wa pensene, molao wa neela go sa tlhole go le teng kgotsa go fokotsega kwa tshwanelo e akanyetsweng, ka nthata ya temana (3), gore ke molao o neelang go pateletsa go tsaya kago kgotsa lefatshe la mongwe. 72

Di tshwanelo tsa lefatshe la temo thuo

(1) Mo mophatong oo— “lefatshe la temo thuo” go raya lefatshe le le dirisiwang kgotsa le siameng mo temo thuo, e tshwana le temo, temo ya moretlwa, temo ya ditlhare, kgotsa temo ya metsi kgotsa go dirisa mo borui, go akaretsa –

(a)

2013

tlhokomelo kgotsa tshola diruiwa, diphologolo tse di tsomiwang, dinonyane tse di ruetsweng nama le mae, phologolo kgotsa notshi; kgotsa

(b) fula kwa diruiwa kgotsa diphologolo tse di tsomiwang;

mme a go akaretse Lefatshe la Magae le lefatshe le mo seeleng sa matoropong le mo lokiesane e butswileng ke molao o amanya go dira lenaneo la toropo le lefatshe ka bo phara kgotsa e supiwang ke molao o amanya molebo wa lefatshe; “lefatshe” go akaretsa sengwe le sengwe se se nnetseruri kgotsa lefatshe la go lema; “karolo ya lefatshe la temo-thuo” go raya karolo ya lefatshe la temo-thuo ikwadisetsweng e le karolo ya lefatshe e kgaogantswe mo Mokwalong wa Molao.

(2) Fa lefatshe la temo-thuo, kgotsa engwe tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego mo lefatsheng lee, la batliwa mo maitlamong a setshaba, le akaretsa—

(a)

motse wa temo-thuo kgotsa mangwe maitlamo;

(b) temogo ya lefatshe, temo ya ditlhare, tshomarelo ya tikologo kgotsa go dirisa go tsomarela diphologolo tsa naga kgotsa di dirisiwa tsa tlholego; kgotsa

(c)

go suta mongwe le mongwe motho o tseelweng ka mabaka a go dirisa lefatshe ka maitlamo a a supiwang ke temana (a) kgotsa (b);

lefatshe, tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego e ka patelesega go tsaywa ke Lefatshe ka kitsiso e beilweng mo di pampiring kgotsa di buka tsa dikgang e supang lefasthe, tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego, morago ga lefatshe, tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego e mo taolong ya Lefatshe ka maemo e tletseng go tswa mo letsatsing la go kgatisa pampiri ya kitsiso.

(3) Fa lefatshe la temo-thuo, kgotsa tshwanelo kgotsa kgatlhego mo lefatsheng lee, kwa patelesega go dira maitlamo a supiwang mo temana (2)—

(a)

tiriso ya mophato 295 temana (1) le wa bobedi(2), ga gona phimola dikeledi ka go duela go hlompha go tsaya, kwantle kwa ga lefatshe le le tokafatsweng pele kwa go tsaya.

(b) ga gona motho o ka ya ko kgotleng go tsaya tshwetso e amanyang go phimolwa dikeledi, kwantle ga phimolo ya dikeledi mo lefatsheng le le tokafatsweng pele ga go tsaywa, gape ga ga gona kgotla e ka kgatlhega go amogela tlhaloso e ntse jana; le

(c) go tsaya lefatshe a kwa tshwanela go botsolotswa ka nthata ya gore go nna le kgethololo mo go thula mophato 56.

(4) Lefatshe lotlhe la temo-thuo le na—

(a) la supiwa mo Rulaganyo ya bosupa mo Molaong Motheo wa pele; kgotsa

(b) la re pele ga le tsatsi la go simolola go tirisa, la lemogiwa ke tiriso ya mophato 16B(2)(a)(ii) kgotsa (iii) mo Molaong Motheo wa pele; go nna le tswelelopele mo maatleng a Lefatshe, gape ga gona phimolo ya dikeledi ka go duela go hlompha go tsaya lefatshe kwantle kwa lefatshe le le tokafatsweng e dirilweng pele kwa go tsaya.

(5) Ka bo nako morago kwa go patelesega go tsaya lefatshe ka tshwanelong mo go diriseng temana (2) , mookamedi o nna le seabe mo ikwadisong ya setlhogo sa lefatshe o tshwanetse go, go sena kitsiso e e kgakajana, go dira ga konokono tetla ya tumelelo mo sengwe le sengwe setlhogo sa mokwalo wa molao le go kwala sengwe le sengwe mo bukeng gore lebaka la go ntsha ka semmuso setlhogo mo mokwalong wa molao le go kwalisa Lefatshe mo setlhogong sa lefatshe.

(6) Molao wa Palamente o tla dira gore go nne moato mo go mongwe le mongwe motho, go sena taolo ya molao, go nna le kgotsa tsaya taolo ya lefatshe la temo-thuo e supiwang mo mophatong oo kgotsa lengwe fela lefatshe la Lefatshe.

(7) Mo akaretsong ya go pateletswa go tsaya lefatshe la temo-thuo ka nthata ya go neela batho lefatshe sesha ka mabaka a thulaganyo aga sesha lefatshe, tse di latelang dingwe tse di thusang di akaretsa ga bofelo le go feta botlhokwa—

(a)

jwa puso ya bokolone batho ba Zimbabwe ba ne tsaelwa lefatshe go na le kgobelelo le tse dingwe di dirisiwa go sena phimola dikeledi;

(b) ka morago kwa go tsaelwa lefatshe batho ba ne ba tsaya dibetsa gore ba boelwe ke lefatshe la bone le go ipusa ka sepolitiki, go ntse jalo ka bofelo kwa tlisa maduo a Boipuso jwa jwa Zimbabwe ka ngwaga wa 1980;

(c) batho ba Zimbabwe tshwanetse go letla go tiisa gape di tshwanelo tsa bone le go boelwa ke bongwa ba bone; le ka go tshwanelong—

(i) puso ya bokolone jwa pele bo na le maikarabelo jwa go duela phimola dikeledi ka go pateletswa lefatshe la temo-thuo le le tserweng go ipaakanya bo nno jwa batho sesha, ka go dirisa madi a butsweng go dira maitlamo aa;

(ii) fa gore puso ya bokolone jwa pele a bokgoni go duela phimola dikeledi ka go dirisa madi aa, Goromente wa Zimbabwe gana maikarabelo jwa go pateletswa go duela phimola dikeledi lefatshe la temo-thuo le nle tserweng go ipaakanya bo nno sesha.

(8) Mophato o o tlhalosa go sena mogopolo wa maikarabelo gore puso ya bokolone ya pele go duela phimola dikeledi tsa lefatshe e supiwang mo mophatong o o supang lefatshe le le tserweng ka maitlamo a go ipaakanya bo nno sesha. 73

Di tshwanelo tsa tikologo

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o na le tshwanelo—

(a) ya tikologo e sena go diphatsa botsogo kgotsa boitekanelo jwa bone; le

(b) go nna le tikologo e pabaletsiwe e nna le maduo a gompieno le a loskia lwa nako e e tlang, ka dirisa mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le tse dingwe di kgato moo

(i) go thibela kgotlelo le tshenyego ya ditshedi;

(ii) go godisa somarelo; le

(iii) go boloka ditshedi go tswelela tlhabololo le tiriso ya di dirisiwa tsa tlholego lobaka go godisiwa ikhonomi le tlhabologo mo go kgathalela matshelo a batho.

(2) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le kgato, e mo selakanyong sa di dirisiwa tse di teng , go fitlha mo tswelelopele ya go temoga di tshwamelo tse di beilweng mo mophatong oo. 74

Kgololego mo go sena lenaneo la teleko

Ga gona motho o ka direlwa teleko mo le lwapeng la gagwe, kgotsa le lwapa la gagwe go thubiwa, go sena taolo ya lekgotla morago kwa go lebelela diemo tsotlhe tse di amanang le tiro. 75 Tshwanelo ya thuto

(1) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi le mo nni wa ruri wa Zimbabwe o nna le tshwanelo—

(a)

2013

ya thuto e botlhokwa e duelwang ke Lefatshe, go akaretsa thuto ya bagolo; le

(b) ya go tswelela le dithuto, fa gore Lefatshe, ka ntlha ya mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le tse dingwe dikgato, le tshwanetse go tsaya tshwetso ya go bona gore go nna le tswelelopele teng gape e bulegileng.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya bula le go itlhokomela, ka go iduelela, mo dikolong tsa thuto tse di ikemitseng ka nosi gape di nna le mabaka a boleng, fa gore ga gona kgethololo ka maemo a kganelwa ke Molao Motheo oo.

(3) Molao o ka tlamela go kwadisiwa kwa dikolo tsa thuto e supiwang ke temana (2) le go tswalwa kwa dikolo tsee fa gore ga di kgotsofatseng mabaka a boleng e laetsweng mo go kwadisa.

(4) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le kgato, e mo selakanyong sa di dirisiwa tse di teng, go fitlha go nna tswelelopele ya go temoga ditshwamelo tse di beilweng mo temana (1). 76

Tshwanelo ya go kgathalela botsogo

(1) Mongwe le mongwe moagedi le mo nnelaruri wa Zimbabwe o nna le tshwanelo ya go bona thuso ya tshedimosetso mo kgathalelong ya botsogo jo botlhokwa, go akaretsa thuso ya go boeletsa kgathalelo ya botsogo.

(2) Mongwe le mongwe motho o tshelang le bolwetse jo bo tsayang sebaka o nna le tshwanelo ya go bona thuso ya tshedimetso mo felong la kgathalelo botsogo jo botlhokwa mo bolwetseng.

(3) Ga gona motho o ka kganelwa go bona kalafi ya tshoganetso ya lengwe lefelong le le kgathelelang botsogo.

(4) Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le kgato, e mo selakanyong sa di dirisiwa tse di teng, go fitlha go nna tswelelopele ya go temoga ditshwanelo tse di beilweng mo mophatong oo. 77

Tshwanelo ya dijo le metsi Mongwe le mongwe motho o nna le tshwanelo ya—

(a)

metsi a bolokesegileng, a phepa le go ikukela; le

(b) dijo jo bo lekaneng;

le Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya mabaka a botlhokwa a molao le kgato, e mo selakanyong sa di dirisiwa tse di teng, go fitlha go nna tswelelopele ya go temoga ditshwamelo tsee. 78

Ditshwanelo tsa go nyala

(1) Mongwe le mongwe motho o fitlhetse di ngwaga tse lesome le borobabobedi o nna le tshwanelo ya go simolola le lwapa.

(2) Ga gona motho o ka pateletswa go tsena mo lonyalong go sena tumelano le maikutlo a gagwe.

(3) Batho ba bong jo bo tshwanang ba a kganelwa go nyalana.

KAROLO 3 Tlhaloso Ya Dingwe Ditshwanelo 79

Tlhaloso ya Karolo ya Boraro

(1) Karolo e e tlhalosa dingwe ditshwanelo le kgololego go bona gore go nna le tlhaloso e ko godimo ya dingwe ditshwanelo le kgololego e kgethang ka kelelelo bangwe batho

(2) Karolo e a ya tshwanela go tlhalosiwa ka ntlha engwe e kganela dingwe ditshwanelo kgotsa kgololego e beilweng mo Karolo ya Bobedi. 80

Ditshwanelo tsa basadi

(1) Mongwe le mongwe mosadi o nna le seriti se se tletseng sa motho se se tshwanang le sa banna gape go akaretsa tshono mo sepolitiki, ikhonomi le tiro ya go kgathalela ba bangwe.

(2) Basadi ba nna le ditshwanelo e tshwanang le ya banna e amana le seabe sa go tlhokomela bana, mme Molao wa Palamente o ka laola gore ditshwanelo tsee di tla dirwa jang.

(3) Yotlhe melao, tlwaelo, tsa tlholego le setso se se dirwang tse di kgoreletsang ditshwanelo tsa basadi tse di supiwang ke Molao Motheo oo go na le phatlha go fitlha go nna kgoreletso. 81

Ditshwanelo tsa bana

(1) Mongwe le mongwe ngwana, go raya mongwe le mongwe mosimane le mosetsana o nna le ngwaga tse kwa tlase kwa lesome le borobabobedi, o nna le tshwanelo—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

(i)

ya kalafi e lekanang ga pele kwa molao, go akaretsa tshwanelo ya go reetswa; ya go neelwa ina le leina la lelwapa (sefane); ya gore fa ke le ngwana o—

(i) tshotswe mo Zimbabwe ; kgotsa
