(3) Matshwawo a seelo sa botlhophi a tshwanetse gore a, ka jalo, kana ko ya go tshwaya go na le palo e lekanang le batlhophi ba ba kwadisetseng mo nngwe le nngwe kgaolo e mo teng kwa Zimbabwe.

(4) Seelo sa dikgotla a tshwanetse gore a, kajalo, kana ko ya go tshwaya go na le palo e lekanang le batlhophi ba ba kwadisetseng mo nngwe le nngwe kgotla mo etelepele a batliwang.

(5) Mo go tshwayeng—

(a)

seelo sa dikgotla, Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetse go tlhomamisa gore a go na kgotla e ka kgaotswang ga bedi kana go feta mo kgotleng ya taulo ya selegae;

(b) seelo sa dikgaolo, Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetse go tlhomamisa gore a go na kgaolo e ka kgaotswang go fitlha e na le kgaolo tse bedi kana go feta;

(6) Mo go kgaoleng Zimbabwe go na le dikgotla le dikgaolo, Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetse, go tlotla nngwe le nngwe kgaolo, go lemoga—

(a)

dintlha tse di bonalang di le botlhokwa;

(c)

seemo sa go abewa kwa batlhophi;

(e)

mo tiragalo ya go tshwaya morago ga go tshwaya kwa ntlha, seelo sa pele sa botlhophi; le

(f)

palo ya batho.

(b) tsela ya dithaeletsano mo teng ga dikgaolo;

(d) kgatlhego ya morafe ga gare ga batlhophi;

Le go neela ditlamorago mo temogo ee, Lekgotla le ka tswa mo tshwetsong ya gore dikgaolo le dikgotla di tshwanetsa go nna le palo ya batlhophi e lekanang, mme ga gona kgaolo kana kgotla e ka nna le palo e ko godimo kwa selekanyo sa masome a mabedi mo lekgolong go ya kwa godimo kana bonye jwa palo ya batlhophi ba ba kwadisetseng go feta engwe kgaolo kana kgotla e tshwanang.

(7) Morago kwa go tshwaya dikgotla le dikgaolo, Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetse go neela Tautona pego ya mo go tlang pele ga tiragalo o na le—

(a)

nankolo ya dikgotla le dikgaolo, mmogo le maina a neetsweng nngwe le nngwe le tlhaloso ya seelo sa teng;

(b) mepe kana dimepe tse di bontsang dikgotla le dikgaolo; le

(c)

nngwe kitso e ntsi kana dintlha tse di bonwang ke Lekgotla di le botlhokwa;

mme Tautona o tshwanetse go dira gore pego ya mo go tlang pele ga tiragalo go neetswe Palamente mo teng kwa malatsi a le bosupa.

(8) Mo teng kwa malatsi a le lesome le bonne morago kwa go neela Palamente pego ya mo go tlang pele ga tiragalo –

(a)

Tautona o ka fetisetsa pego gape mo Lekgotleng la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) go akanya mo nngwe le nngwe kgang kgotsa silo;

(b) nngwe le nngwe Kgotla ya Palamente e ka tsaya tshwetso ya gore pego e e tshwanetse go fetisetswa gape ko Lekgotleng la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) go akanya mo go tletseng nngwe le nngwe kgang kana silo, mme fa go ntse jaloTautona o tshwanetse go fetisetsa pego gape ko Lekgotleng go akanya mo go tletseng.

(9) Fa pego ya mo go tlang pele ga tiragalo ya go tshwaya e fetisetswe morago ko Lekgotleng ga tlase kwa temana 8, Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetse go akanya mo go tletseng mo kgangeng ka silong se siamanang, mme tshwetso ya Lekgotla ke ya bofelo.

(10) Ka bonako morago kwa go obamelwa ke temana (7) le (9), Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) le tshwanetsa go naya pego ya go tshwaya ya bofelo mo go Tautona.

(11) Mo teng ga malatsi a le lesome le bonne morago kwa go amogela Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission) pego ya bofelo, Tautona o tshwanetse go taola gore e pegewe moKorateng go tsibisa maina le dielo tsadi kgotla le dikgaolo gore ke tshwetso ya bofelo e kaetsweng ke Lekgotla.

(12) Fa go na le ngongorego ga gare ga go tlhalosa dielo tsa nngwe le nngwe kgotla kana kgaolo le dimepe e baakantswe ke Lekgotla la Ditlhopho la Zimbabwe (Zimbabwe Electoral Commission), tlhaloso ya yema e le yone e botlhokwatlhokwa.

KGAOLO 8 Baatlhodi Le Di Kgotla KAROLO 1 Tsebeletso Ya Lekhotla 162 Taolo ya boatlhodi Bolaodi jwa boatlhodi bo tswa mo bathong ba Zimbabwe mme bo neetswe dikgotla tse di latelang—

(a)

Kgotla la Molao Motheo;

(c)

Kgotla Kgolo;

(e)

Kgotla ya Tsamaiso;

(b) Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa;

(d) Kgotla ya Babereki;

(f)

kgotla ya magastrata;

(g) kgotla ya twaelo; le

(h) dingwe dikgotla tse di bupilweng kwa tlase kwa Molao wa Palamente.

163 Boatlhodi

(1) Boatlhodi jwa Zimbabwe bo na le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo le baatlhodi ba Kgotla Molao Motheo;

(b) baatlhodi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa;

(c)

Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo le baatlhodi bangwe ba kgotla ya teng;

(d) Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla ya Babereki le baatlhodi bangwe ba kgotla ya teng;

(e)

Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla ya Tsamaiso le baatlhodi bangwe ba kgotla ya teng; le

(f)

baeteledipele ba kgotla ya magastrata, kgotla ya twaelo le tse dingwe dikgotla tse di bupilweng ke kana kwa tlase kwa Molao wa Palamente.

(2) Moatlhodi-Mogolo ke tlhogo ya boatlhodi wa laola Kgotla ya Molao Motheo le Kgotla e e Botlhokwatlhokwa.

(3) Tautona wa Baatlhodi ba Kgotla Kgolo wa laola kgotla ya teng.

(4) Tautona wa Baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Babereki wa laola kgotla ya teng.

(5) Tautona wa Baatlhodi ba Kgotla Tsamaiso wa laola kgotla ya teng. 164 Boipuso jwa Boatlhodi

(1) Dikgotla di nna le boipuso mme di obamela Molao Motheo oo fela le molao, ka tekatekanyo, ka bonako mme go sena go tshoga, kgetha kana kgobatso.

(2) Boipuso, tekatekanyo le boitseanape ya dikgotla ke se se ga gare jwa puso ya molao le puso ya batho ka batho, mme fa go ntse jalo—

(a)

fa go se Lefatshe kana lekgotla kana setlhopha sa goromente, le mongwe motho, o ka kgoreletsa tiro ya dikgotla;

(b) Lefatshe, ka go dirisa molao le tse dingwe dikgato, le tshwanetse go thusa le pabalela dikgotla go tlhomamisa gore di nna le boipuso, tekatekanyo, seriti, kamogelo le boitseanape le go tlhomamisa gore tsa latela ditsamaiso se se beilweng mo mophato 165 .

(3) Taelo kgotsa tshwetso ya kgotla ya bofa Lefatshe le batho botlhe le ma kgotla a goromente le setlhopha mo go tirisiweng teng, mme di tshwanetse go tlotla se se dirilweng ke dikgotla.

(4) Ga gona sengwe mo mophatong oo se se ka tlhalosiwa se le se kgoreletse sa Molao wa Palamente go dira tiro ya go tlhamalatsa tiro ya le loko la boatlhodi, fa go ntse jalo a kwa tlamela go kgoreletsa boipuso jwa mmereki wa boatlhodi o amanang le go dira tiro ya boatlhodi le go kgoreletsa boipuso jwa boatlhodi botlhe fela 165 Tsamaiso ya Boatlhodi

(1) Mo go direng taolo ya boatlhodi, maloko a boatlhodi a tshwanetse go kgakololwa ke ditsamaiso tse di latelang—

(a)

go lekanetsa go tshwanetse go dirwa mo go botlhe batho, go sena go lebelela maemo a motho;

(b) go lekanetsa a kwa tshwanela go diega, le ka bofelo maloko a boatlhodi a tshwanetse go dira tiro ya bone sentle le maikaelelo a go dira ka bonako;

(c)

seabe sa dikgotla ke silo se se botlhokwa mo go pabaleleng ditshwanelo le kgololego ya batho le puso ya batho ka batho;

(2) Maloko a boatlhodi, a le nosi kana a kopantswe, a tshwanetse go tlotla le go hlompha ofisi ya bone ya boatlhodi gore morafe o na le tshepo mo go bone mme ba tshwanetse go leka go tsholetsa boipuso ba bone go tsweledisa tshepamo ya morafe mo boatlhodeng.

(3) Mo go tsayeng tshwetso ya boatlhodi, le loko la boatlhodi o tshwanetse go dira ka kgololesego mme go sena kgoreletso kana pateletso.

(4) Maloko a boatlhodi a wa tshwanele—

(a)

go tsena mo dino tsa sepolotiki;

(c)

go kopa madi kgotsa thusa senngwe le senngwe setlhopha sa sepolotiki; kgotsa

(b) go tshwara ofisi mo kana na le maloko ya senngwe le senngwe setlhopha sa sepolitiki;

(d) go tsena mo phuthegong ya sepolitiki.

(5) Maloko a boatlhodi a wa tshwanela go kopa kana amogela nngwe le nngwe mpho, lefa, madi a kadimo kana somarela ntlha enngwe fela e tla pateletsa gore maitswaro a boatlhodi kana gore go bonagale gore boatlhodi jo bo maswe.

(6) Maloko a boatlhodi a tshwanetse go neela tiro ya bone ko pele go feta tse dingwe dilo, mme a ba tshwanele go tsena mo mephatong e tla fapana le go kgoreletsa tiro ya bone yo boatlhodi.

(7) Maloko a boatlhodi a tshwanetse go tsaya mabaka a go tlhokomela le go netefatsa kitso ya tiroya bone, bokgoni le boleng jwa bone, mme ba tshwanetse lemoga phetogo e diragalang se gae le molao wa mafatshefatshe. 166 Lekgotla la Molao Motheo

(1) Kgotla ya Molao Motheo ke kgotla e botlhokwa mo tlhokomeleng mme e na le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo le Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo; le

(b) baatlhodi ba le batlhano ba Kgotla ya Molao Motheo;

(2) Fa tiro ya motlhatlhama setilo sa moatlhodi wa batliwa ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo ka nako e lekanetsweng, Moatlhodi-Mogolo o ka tlhopha moatlhodi mongwe kgotsa moatlhodi wa pele go tlhatlhama setilo sa ga moatlhodi wa Kgotla ya Molao Motheo ka nako ya teng.

(3) Tsheko tse di dirwang ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo—

(a)

mabapi le go kgoreletsa botlhokwa jwa tshwanelo kana kgololego ya batho se se beilweng mo Kgaolo 4, mabapi le tlhopho ya ga Tautona kgotsa Mothusa-Tautona, e tshwanetse go reetsiwa ke baatlhodi botlhe ba Kgotla;

(b) kwa ntle kwa tsheko e beilweng mo temana (a), e tshwanetse go reetsiwa ke ke baatlhodi ba le bararo ba Kgotla; mme Molao wa Palamente kana melawana ya Kgotla e ka tlamela kgang ya puisanyo go reetsiwa ke moatlhodi mongwe kana ba le ba ntsi.

(4) Baatlhodi kgotsa baatlhodi ba pele ba ba tlhophilweng go tlhatlhama setilo kwa tlase kwa temana (2) ba ka tswelela go nna ba le baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Molao

Motheo morago kwa go fela jwa sebaka, ka ntlha ya go etsa nngwe le nngwe tsheko e simolotse pele kwa go fela kwa sebaka sa go tlhatlhama setilo. 167 Maatla a Lekgotla la Molao Motheo

(1) Kgotla ya Molao Motheo—

(a)

ke kgotla e kwa godimodimo mo dikgangeng tsotlhe tsa molao motheo, mme tshwetso ya yone e bofa dikgotla tsotlhe;

(b) ya tsaya tshwetso mo dikgangeng tsa molao motheo fela le tse dingwe tse di amanang le megopolo mo kgangeng tsa molao motheo, go lebelwe thata le thaloso kwa tlase kwa mophato 131(8)(b) le temana 9(2) ya Thulaganyo ya Botlhano; le

(c)

go tsaya tshwetso ya bofelo ya gore kgang ke kgang ya molao motheo kana kabo ee e amana le mogopolo wa kgang ya molao motheo.

(2) Fa go latelwa Molao Motheo oo, Kgotla ya Molao Motheo ke yona fela e ka—

(a)

kgakolola gore molao o batliwang go agiwa ke molao motheo, mme e ka dira jalo fa gore ba ba amnang le go aga molao ba laetswe go ya mo go yona se se mo Molaong Motheo oo;

(b) reetsa le go laola ngangisano e amana le tlhopho ya go tsena ko ofising ya ga Tautona;

(c)

reetsa le go laola ngangisano e amana le gore motho o na le boitseanape kana gana go tshwara ofisi ya Mothusa-Tautona; le

(d) laola gore Palamente kana Tautona ofeitse go kgotsofatsa maikaelelo a molao motheo.

(3) Kgotla ya Molao Motheo ya tsaya tshwetso ya bofelo gore Molao wa Palamente kana maitshwaro a ga Tautona kana Palamente a mo Molaong Motheo, mme e tshwanetse go rurifatsa nngwe le nngwe taolo ya go sa tetlelela molao motheo e tseyweng ke engwe kgotla pele kwa dira taolo ee.

(4) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela gore Kgotla ya Molao Motheo go dira boatlhodi mme ka ntlha ya silo se e ka ineela maatla go dira melwana ya kgotla.

(5) Melawana ya Kgotla ya Molao Motheo e tshwanele go dumela motho, fa gore kgatlego ya go lekanetsa mme go na kana go sena go tlogela Kgotla ya Molao Motheo—

(a)

go tlhamalala go ya ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo go tlisa kgang ya molao motheo;

(b) go tlhamalala go ya ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo go tlisa botsolotso;

(c)

go bonala ka re o tsala ya kgotla.

168 Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa

(1) Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa ke kgotla e botlhokwa ya tlhokomelo mme e nna le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo le Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo;

(c)

ba bangwe baatlhodi b aba tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa temana (2).

(b) baatlhodi ba ba fetang babedi mo Kgotleng e Botlhokwatlhokwa; le

(2) Fa kwa batliwa o mongwe moatlhodi ko Kgotleng e Botlhokwatlhokwa nako e lekanetsweng, Moatlhodi-Mogolo o ka tlhopha moatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo, kgotsa

moatlhodi wa pele go tlhatlhama setilo sa ga moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa ka nako ya teng.

(3) Baatlhodi kgotsa baatlhodi ba pele ba ba tlhophilweng go tlhatlhama setilo kwa tlase kwa temana (2) b aka tswelela go nna ban tse ba le baatlhodi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa morago kwa go fela kwa nako ya tiro ya bone, ka ntlha ya go dira nngwe le nngwe tsheko e simolotse ka nako ya go tlhatlhama setilo. 169 Basekisi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa

(1) Kgotla e Botlhokwtlhokwa ke kgotla ya bofelo ya go botsolotsa mo Zimbabwe, kwa ntle kwa kgang e mo boatlhodeng ba Kgotla ya Molao Motheo.

(2) Se se obamelwa ke temana (1), Molao wa Palamente o ka neela bongwe boatlhodi le maatla ko Kgotleng e Botlhokwatlhokwa.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela gore go dirwe boatlhodi ke Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa mme ka mabaka aa e ka neela maatla go dira melawana ya kgotla.

(4) Melawana ya kgotla e ka kopa mokwaledi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa nngwe le nngwe boatlhodi ba Kgotla le maatla mo tshekong ya morafe—

(a)

gore taolo mo tshekong e senang ngangisano, kwa ntle kwa taolo e kgoreletsa seemo kana tshireletso kana tlhokomelo ya bana;

(b) go tsaya tshwetso kgotsa kgang ya puisanyo, go akaretsa tlhaloso ya bokaedi, mme e seng kgang e kgoreletsa kgololesego ya mongwe le mongwe motho; mme melawna mongwe le mongwe motho o kgoreletsweng ke taolo ya mokwaledi kana tshwetso go neelwa tshwanelo ya potsolotso ke moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa, o ka irurifatsa, ipaakanya kana emisa kana neela engwe taolo kana tshwetso. 170 Kgotla Kgolo Kgotla Kgolo ke kgotla e botlhokwa ya tlhokomelo mme e na le—

(a)

Moatlhodi -Mogolo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo le Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo; le

(b) ba bangwe baatlhodi ba Kgotla Kgolo mme ba ka tlhophiwa nako le nako. 171 Basekisi ba Kgotla Kgolo

(1) Kgotla Kgolo—

(a)

e na le boatlhodi jwa ntlha mo kgangeng tsotlhe tsa morafe le tsa borukutlhi mo Zimbabwe ka bo phara;

(b) e na le boatlhodi jwa go kaela dikgotla tsa magastrata le tse dingwe dikgotla tse di kwa tlsae kwa yone le go potsolotsa tshwetso tsa tsone ;

(c) e ka tsaya tshwetso mo dikgangeng tsa molao motheo kwa ntle kwa dikgang tshwetso e ka tsaywang ke Kgotla ya Molao Motheo; le

(d) e ka amogela potsolotso ya boatlhodi fa e ka neelwa ke Molao wa Palamente.

(2) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela gore Kgotla Kgolo e dire boatlhodi le go ineela maatla go aga melawana ya kgotla ya teng.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela Kgolo go kgaoganya mo di karolo tse di kgethegileng, mme nngwe le nngwe karolo e tshwanetse go dira tiro yotlhe fela ya boatlhodi jwa Kgotla Kgolo nngwe le nnwge kgang e tlang mo go yone.

(4) Melawana ya kgotla e ka neela mokwaledi wa Kgotla Kgolo maatla mo tshekong tsa morafe—

(a)

gore taolo mo tshekong e senang ngangisano, kwa ntle kwa taolo e kgoreletsa seemo kana tshireletso kana tlhokomelo ya bana;

(b) go tsaya tshwetso kgotsa kgang ya puisanyo, go akaretsa tlhaloso ya bokaedi, mme e seng kgang e kgoreletsa kgololesego ya mongwe le mongwe motho; mme melawna mongwe le mongwe motho o kgoreletsweng ke taolo ya mokwaledi kana tshwetso go neelwa tshwanelo ya potsolotso ke moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Kgolo, o ka irurifatsa, ipaakanya kana emisa kana neela engwe taolo kana tshwetso. 172 Kgotla ya babereki

(1) Kgotla ya babereki ke kgotla ya tlhokomelo mme e na le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo;

(b) ba bangwe baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Babereki b aka tlhophiwa nako le nako. [Temana e baakantswe ka teme 3 wa Molao 10 wa 2017]

(2) Kgotla ya Babereki e na le boatlhodi mo dikgangeng tsa tiro le mmereko e ka neelwa ke Molao wa Palamente.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela gore Kgotla ya Babereki e dire boatlhodi le go ineela maatla go aga melawana ya kgotla ya teng. 173 Kgotla ya Tsamaiso

(1) Kgotla ya Tsamaiso ke kgotla ya tlhokomelo mme e na le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo; le”.

(b) ba bangwe baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Tsamaiso ba ka tlhophiwa nako le nako. [Temana e baakantswe ka teme 4 wa Molao 10 wa 2017]

(2) Kgotla ya Tsamaisoe na le boatlhodi mo dikgangeng tsa tsamaiso mme e ka ineelwa ke Molao wa Palamente.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela gore Kgotla ya Tsamaiso e dire boatlhodi le go ineela maatla go aga melawana ya kgotla ya teng. 174 Dingwe dikgotla le lekgotla le le hapegileng

(1) Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela pupo, thamo le boatlhodi ba—

(a)

kgotla ya ka magastrata, go tsaya tshwetso mo tshekong ya morafe le ya borukutlhi;

(b) kgotla ya molao ya setso ka gore boatlhodi e na le sa ntlha jwa go dira molao wa setso;

(c)

dingwe dikgotla tse di kwa tlase kwa Kgotla Kgolo; le

(d) lekgotla la go tsaya tshwetso, puisanyo le tse dingwe di ngangisanyo gore go nne le tirisanyo mmogo.

(2) Fa go latelwa mophato 188(4) ya Molao Motheo, madi a tuelo le tse dingwe tse di duelang baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Tsamaiso a kwa tshwanela go fokotswa ba ntse ba le mo tirong kana fa ba emetse yo mongwe go tswa mo letsatsing la paakanyo. [Temana e baakantswe ka teme 5 wa Molao 10 wa 2017]

175 Maatla a dikgotla mo dikgangeng tsa molao motheo

(1) Fa kgotla e dirile taolo e amana le go fapana jwa tetlelesego ya molao motheo ya mongwe le mongwe molao kana mangwe le mangwe maitshwaro a ga Tautona kana Palamente, taolo ee a ye na maatla ga se irurifatswe ke Kgotla ya Molao Motheo.

(2) Kgotla e dirang taolo e fapana le tetlelesego ya molao motheo se se beilweng mo temana (1) e ka neela tlogelo ka nako e khutshwane kana kgololesego e khutshwane ya mongwe, kana emisa tsheko, go emela tshwetso ya Kgotla ya Molao mo tetlelesego ya molao kana maitshwaro a amanang.

(3) Motho o na le kgatlhego e lekaneng o ka botsolotsa, kana etsa, ka tlhamalalo ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo go rurifatsa kana farologanya taolo e amanang le tetlelesego ya molao motheo ke kgotla se se beilweng mo temana (1).

(4) Fa kgang ya molao motheo ya tswa mo nngwe le nngwe tsheko ko kgotleng, moetedipele wa kgotla yone o ka le, fa go ntse jalo ka kopo ya mongwe le mongwe motho o mo tshekong, o tshwanetse go tsamaisa kgang ee ko Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo ga se fa a bona gore kopo ee ke go tsameka fela kana e tletse mathata.

(5) Molao wa Palamente kana melawana ya kgotla e tshwanetse go tlamela mogopolo wa go yak o Kgotleng ya Moalo Motheo mo taolo e amanang le go fapana le tetlelesego ya molao motheo e dirilwe se se beilweng mo temana (1) ke kgotla e seng Kgotla ya Molao Motheo. e ka—

(6) Mo go tsayeng tshwetso ya kgang ya molao motheo mo boatlhodeng kgotla

(a)

tsibisa ka phatlhalatso monngwe le monngwe molao kana maitshwaro o na le phetogo mo Molaong Motheo a wa dumelwa ka bogolo jwa phetogo ya teng;

(b) dira nngwe le nngwe taolo e lekaneng le e na le tekatekanyo, go akaretsa taolo e lekanetsang ditlamorago tse di amanang le kitsiso ya phatlhalatsa ya go fapana le tetlelesego le taolo e emisang ka seemo kana go sena seemo sa kitsiso ya go fapana le tetlelesego mo sengwe le sengwe sebaka go dumela moitseanape wa taolo go baakanya tshenyo ee. 176 Maatla magolo a Kgotla ya Molao Motheo, Kgotla e Botlhokwatlokwa le Kgotla Kgolo Kgotla ya Molao Motheo, Kgotla e Botlhokwatlokwa le Kgotla Kgolo di na le maatla a tshireletso le go laola ditsamaiso tsa bone le go simolola molao kgotsa molao wa ngwao, go lebelwe thata dikgatlhego tsa tokamo le di tlamelo tsa Molao Motheo. KAROLO 2 Tlhopho Ya Baatlhodi Le Sebaka Sa Go Bereka Ga Bone 177 Ditlhoko tsa baathlodi ba Kgotla ya Molao Motheo

(1) Motho o ka tshwanela go hiriwa jaaka Moatlhodi mo Kgotleng ya Molao Motheo fa a ale moagi wa Zimbabwe, a le dingwaga tse masome a mane go ya godimo gape a nna le kitso ka molao wa Molao Motheo gape, godimo ga mo, go tshwanetse go nne le tse di latelang—

(a)

o kile a bereka ele moatlhodi o nna le boikarabelo jo bo fitlheletseng mo go tsa di tshenyo mo lefatsheng la molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; kgotsa

(b) ka ditlhoko tse ditshwanetseng tse di nne tsa dira gore a kgone go bereka a le moatlhodi ga di ngwaga di le lesome le bobedi go ya godimo, kgotsa go bereka mo go ile ga tswelela ka go tlhomagana ga dingwaga—

(i) mo Zimbabwe; kgotsa

(ii) mo le fatsheng le molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; gape a santse a ka bereka.

(2) Go hirwa o le moatlhodi wa lekgotla la Molao Motheo motho o tshwanetse go ba a siametse tiro eo. 178 Ditlhoko tsa baatlhodi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa

(1) Motho o ka tshwanela go hiriwa jaaka Moatlhodi mo Kgotleng e Botlhokwatlhokwa fa a ale moagi wa Zimbabwe, a le dingwaga tse masome a mane le, godimo ga mo—

(a)

o kile a bereka ele moatlhodi o nna le boikarabelo jo bo fitlheletseng mo go tsa di tshenyo mo lefatsheng le molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; kgotsa

(b) ka ditlhoko tse ditshwanetseng tse di nne tsa dira gore a kgone go bereka a le moatlhodi ga di ngwaga di le lesome go ya godimo, kgotsa go bereka mo go ile ga tswelela ka go tlhomagana ga dingwaga(i) mo Zimbabwe; kgotsa

(ii) mo le fatsheng la molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; gape a santse a ka bereka.

(2) Go hirwa o le moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa motho o tshwanetse go ba a siametse tiro eo. 179 Ditlhoko tsa baatlhodi ba Kgotla Kgolo, Kgotla ya Babereki le Kgotla ya Tsamaiso

(1) Motho o ka tshwanela go hiriwa jaaka Moatlhodi mo Kgotleng Kgolo, Kgotla ya Babereki le Kgotla ya Tsamaiso fa a ale moagi wa Zimbabwe, a le dingwaga tse masome a mane le, godimo ga mo—

(a)

o kile a bereka ele moatlhodi o nna le boikarabelo jo bo fitlheletseng mo go tsa di tshenyo mo lefatsheng la molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; kgotsa

(b) ka ditlhoko tse ditshwanetseng tse di nne tsa dira gore a kgone go bereka a le moatlhodi ga di ngwaga di le lesome go ya godimo,kgotsa go bereka mo go ile ga tswelela ka go tlhomagana ga dingwaga—

(i) mo Zimbabwe; kgotsa

(ii) mo le fatsheng la molao wa lona ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa se le puo ya lefatshe; gape a santse a ka bereka.

(2) Go hirwa o le moatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo, le Kgotla ya Babereki le Kgotla ya Tsamaiso motho o tshwanetse go ba siametse tiro eo.

180 Go tlhophiwa kwa baatlhodi

(1) Moathlodi-Mogolo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo, Mmueledi- Mogolo wa Kgotla Kgolo ba tlhophiwa ke Tautona se se beilweng mo mophatong oo.

(2) Moathlodi-Mogolo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Moathlodi le Mmueledi-Mogolo wa Kgotla Kgolo le baatlhodi bangwe ba tla tlhophiwa ke Tautona morago kwa go puisana le Lekgotla ya Dikgotla.

(3) Moathlodi-Mogolo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Moathlodi le Mmueledi-Mogolo wa Kgotla Kgolo a kwa tsamailana ke se se builweng ke Lekgotla la Dikgotla fa go latelwa temana (2), Tautona o tla taola gore Senethe e pegelwe ka bonako: Gore go tlhamalale, go bofilwe gore se se dirilwe ke Tautona ke gone o ko mo molaong mme a kwa tshwanela go fetolwa.

(4) Fa go na le tiragalo ya go tlhopha Moatlhodi-Mogolo, Mothusa MoatlhodiMogolo, Mmueledi-Mogolo ba Kgotla Kgolo, Lekgotla la Dikgotla le tshwanetse go—

(a)

go phatlhalatsa maemo:

(b) go pegela Tautona le setshaba go itlhophela batho;

(c)

go etsa go puisana le ba simololang tiro;

(e)

go neela maina a bafenyi mo go Tautona fa go dirisiwa temana (5), Tautona o tshwanetse go tlhopha mofenyi mongwe go tsaya setilo.

(d) go kwala maina a bafenyi ba le bararo ba ba ka tsayang setilo; le

(5) Fa Tautona a bona gore mo maineng a neitsweng fa go dirisiwa temana

(4)(e) go sena o ka tsayang setilo, o tshwanetse go tlhopha a mangwe maina a le mararo mo Kgotleng ya Dikgotla, mme Tautona o tshwanetse go tlhopha mongwe fela go tsaya setilo.

(6) Tautona o tshwanetse go dira gore go pegewe mo Korateng go tsibisa setshaba se se diragalang go tsaya setilo fa go dirisiwa mophato oo.

(7) Setilo sa ga moatlhodi-mogolo wa Kgotla ya Babereki le setilo sa ga moatlhodi-mogolo wa Kgotla ya Tsamaiso tse di farologaneng le Bonni jwa batho go tshwanetse go neelwa yo mongwe moatlhodi kana motlhatlama setilo sa ga moatlhodi, mme o tshwanetse go tlhophiwa ke Moatlhodi-Mogolo moargo kwa go puisana le Lekgotla ya Dikgotla”. [Temana e baakantswe ka teme 6 wa Molao 10 wa 2017]

181 Ditlhopho tsa baetsa-boatlhodi

(1) Fa go na le phatlha mo ofising ya ga Moatlhodi-Mogolo kana motshwari wa ofisi ga kgoni go dira tiro ya ofisi, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo wa tlhatlhama setilo sa gagwe, mme fa gore di ofisi tseopedi ga gona ope kgotsa ga di kgoni go dira tiro ya tsone, mongwe moatlhodi o mogolo o latelang wa Kgotla ya Molao Moltheo wa tlhatlhama setilo sa ga Moatlhodi-Mogolo.

(2) Fa ofisi ya—

(a) Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo;

(b) moatlhodi-mogolo wa Kgotla ya Babereki; kana

(c) moatlhodi-mogolo wa Kgotla ya Tsamaiso”; e na le phatlha kana motshwari wa ofisi ga kgoni go dira tiro ya ofisi yone, mongwe moatlhodi o mogolo wa kgotla ya teng wa tlhatlhama setilo sa ga Tautona wa Baatlhodi. [Temana e baakantswe ka teme 7 wa Molao 10 wa 2017]

(3) Fa kwa tlhokagala gore go oketsa le mongwe moatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo, Kgotla ya Babereki kgotsa Kgotla ya Tsamaiso wa batliwa ka nako e lekanetsweng Tautona, wa tsaya kgato ka kgakgololo ya Lekgotla la Baatlhodi, ya go tlhopha moatlhodi wa pele go tlhatlhama setilo ka kgwedi tse di sa feteng lesome le bobedi, sebaka se se ka oketsewa gape ka tse dingwe dikgwedi di le lesome le bobedi kwa nngwe fela.

(4) Batho ba ba tlhatlhama setilo kwa tlase kwa temana (3) ba ka tswelela go nna ba le baatlhodi morago ga go fela ga sebaka sa bone, ka ntlha ya go dira nngwe le nngwe tsheko e simolotse pele kwa bone ba ntse ba le sebakeng sa go tlhatlama setilo. 182 Go tlhophiwa ga bo mmakasetarata le maloko a baatlhodi Molao wa Palamente o ka tlamela go tlhophiwa kwa bo mmakasetarata le bangwe babereki ko boatlhodeng ba ba seng baatlhodi, mme—

(a)

bo mmakasetrata ba tshwaentse go tlhophiwa ke Lekgotla la Baatlhodi;

(b) ba bangwe babereki ko boatlhondeng ba ba seng bo mmakasterata kana baatlhodi ba tshwanetse go tlhophiwa ka ruriatso ya Lekgotla la Baatlhodi;

(c)

tsotlhe ditlhopho di tshwanetse go tiragala mo pontshong le go sena tshogo, kgethololo le kgobatso kgotsa somarela ntlha engwe.

183 Baatlhodi ba tshwanetse go tlhophiwa mo kgotleng engwe fela Kwa ntle ga e seng jalo kwa tlamelwa ke Molao Motheo oo, motho a a tshwanela go tlhophiwa mo di kgotkleng tse di fetang e nngwe fela. 184 Dikamano tsa boatlhodi go bonagatsa setshaba Ditlhopho tsa boatlhodi di tshwanetse go bonagatsa mefutafuta le pupo ya bong jwa Zimbabwe. 185 Maikano a ofisi

(1) Pele kwa go simolola tiro ko ofisi Moatlhodi-Mogolo kgota Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo, o tshwanetse go tsaya, kwa pele kwa ga Tautona kgotsa mongwe o neetswe taolo ke Tautona, ikano ya boatlhodi se se beilweng mo Thulaganyo ya Boraro.

(2) Pele kwa go simolola tiro ko ofising moatlhodi, e seng Moatlhodi-Mogolo kgotsa Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo o tshwanetse go tsaya, pele kwa Moatlhodi-Mogolo kana mongwe moatlhodi mogolo o latelang o teng, ikano ya boatlhodi se se beilweng mo Thulaganyo ya Boraro.

(3) Mmakasterata le ba bangwe babereki baotlhodi, bangwe baatlhodi ka gore ba kwa tlase kwa Melao ya Palamente, ka go tlhophiwa e tshwanetse go laela ikano e ka tsaywang ke one maloko a boatlhodi. 186 Sebaka sa boatlhodi

(1) Baatlhodi ba le Kgotla la Molao Motheo ba tlhophiwa dingwaga di lesome le botlhano fela, mme—

(a) mme ba batshwanetse go ithola marapo ba fitlha ngwaga tse di masome a le bosupa; mme

(b) morago kwa go fetsa sebaka sa bone, ba ka tlhophiwa go nna baatlhodi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwtlhokwa kgotsa Kgotla Kgolo, ka go itlhophela, fa ba siametse tlhopho ee.

(2) Boatlhodi ba Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa, Kgotla Kgolo le mongwe le mongwe Moatlhodi wa tshwara ofisi ka kgwedi ya go simolola tiro ko ofising go fitlha a nna le ngwaga tse di masome a le bosupa, nako ya go ithola marapo.

(3) Motho o ka tlhophiwa go nna moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa, Kgotla Kolo le tse dingwe dikgotla ka naoko e bofilweng, mme fa motho a tlhophilwe, kwa ntle kwa go tlhtlhama setilo, wa ema go nna moatlhodi ka go fitlha ngwaga tse di masome a le bosupa kana sebaka sa gagwe sa go tlhophiwa se sa ntse se le teng.

(4) Fa gore moatlhodi o itholile kgotsa o fitlhetse ngwaga tse di masome a le bosupa kgotsa, mo kgangeng ya moatlhodi wa Kgotla ya Molao Motheo kgotsa moatlhodi o buiwang ka yena mo temana (3), o fitlhetse pheto ya sebaka sa gagwe sa ofisi, o ka tswelela go nna a le moatlhodi ka ntlha ya go dira nngwe le nngwe tsheko e simolotse pele kwa ene ka nako a ntse a le moatlhodi.

(5) Mongwe le mongwe moatlhodi o ka ithola marapo ko ofising ya gagwe nngwe le nngwe nako ka go kwala kitsiso ya phatlhalatsa ko go Tautona ka go tirisa Komiti ya Boatlhodi.

(6) Ofisi ya moatlhodi a ya tshwanela go emesiwa ka ntata ya fela kwa sebaka sa gagwe ko ofising. 187 Go tlosewa jwa baatlhodi

(1) Moatlhodi o ka tlosewa ko ofising fa gore—

(a)

wa palelwa ke go dira tiro ya gagwe ya ko ofising ka ntlha, ya go tlhokabokgoni jwa tlhaloganyo kgotsa mmele;

(b) wa tlhoka boitseanape thata;

(c)

o senyetse thata;

mme moatlhodi a ka ke a tlosiwa ko ofising kwa ntle kwa go tirisiwa mophato oo.

(2) Fa Tautona a ka akanya gore kgang ya ga tlosa Moatlhodi-Mogolo ko ofising e tshwanetse go tlhotlhomisiwa, Tautona o ka tlhopha setlhopha go senkasenka mo kgangeng ee.

(3) Fa Komiti ya Boatlhodi la gakolola Tautona kgang ya go tlosa mongwe moatlodi, mmogo le Moatlhodi-Mogolo, ko ofising go tshwanetse go tlhotlhomisiwa, Tautona o ka tlhopha setlhopha go senkasenka mo kgangeng ee.

(4) Setlhopha se se tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa mophato oo se tshwanetse go na le maloko a le mararo a tlhophilwe ke Tautona, go na le—

(a)

mongwe wa bone o tshwanetse go le—

(i) motho o berekile a le moatlhodi wa Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa kgotsa Kgotla Kgotlo mo Zimbabwe; kgotsa

(ii) motshwari kana motshwari wa pele wa ya ofisi a le moatlhodi wa Kgotla o senang

selekanyo sa boatlhodi jwa kgangeng ya morafe kana ya borukhutlhi mo lefatshe la ena molao wa teng ke Roman-Dutch kgotsa Sekgoa, mme Sekgoa sa lemogiwa ke le puo e tirisiwang semmuso;

(b) mongwe wa bone o tshwanetse go tlhophiwa mo nankolong ya bararo kana go feta mo ba berekang ko molao ngwaga di le bosupa ka go latelana kana go feta o tlhophilweng ke kgobogano, e kwa tlase kwa Molao wa Palamente, le le emela ba babereki ba molao mo Zimbabwe.

(5) Kgobogano e buiwang ka yone mo temana (4)(b) e tshwanetse go dira nankolo e buiwang mo temana eo fa e batliwa ke Tautona.

(6) Tautona o tshwnetse go tlhopha mongwe wa maloko wa setlhopha se se tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa mophato oo gore a nne mudalasetilo wa setlhopha.

(7) Setlhopha se se tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa temana (2) kgotsa (3) se tshwanetse go senkasenka mo kgangeng ya go tlosa moatlhodi wa teng ko ofising le, fa e dirile jalo, e tshwanetse go bulela Tautona e se fitlhetse le tshwetso ya gore moatlhodi o tshwanetse go tlosewa ko ofising kana nyaa.

(8) Tautona o tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ka go latela tshwetso yay a setlhopha se se beilweng mo temana (7) .

(9) Setlhopha se se tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa mophato oo o na le le ditshwanelo le maatla a tshwanang le balaodi kwa tlase kwa Molao wa Kgobogano la Balaodi [Kgaolo 10:07], kana mengwe melao e emisang Molao oo.

(10) Fa kgang ya go tlosa moatlhodi ko ofising e tsamaile ko setlhopheng kwa tlase kwa mophato oo, moatlhodi wa emesiwa ko ofisi go fitlha Tautona, ka tshwesto ya setlhopha, wa ntsha kemiso kgotsa tlosa moatlhodi ko ofising.

(11) Molao wa Palamente o ka tsholetsa Komiti ya Boatlhodi kana setlhopha se se tlhophilweng kwa tlase kwa mophato oo go batla gore moatlhodi a tlise tlhatlhobo ya botsogo ko kgoboganong la botsogo le bupilweng ka maikaelelo ya gone, go rurifatsa botsogo jwa mmele kana tlhaloganyo. 188 Maemo le sebaka sa tiro ya baatlhodi

(1) Baatlhodi ba le tetla ya kamogelo, madi a itshetso le nngwe thuso e bofelweng nako le nako ke Komiti ya Boatlhodi ka go rurifatswa ke Tautona e neelwang ka go puisana le Tona o amanang le go lekanetsa le kgakololo ya Tona wa madi.

(2) Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go tlamela maemo a a go tiro kwa babereki ba boatlhodi kwa ntle kwa baatlhodi le o tshwanetse go bona gore baa tsholetswa, tsamaiswa kwa mangwe ma felo, le go ntshewa, le nngwe le nngwe paakanyo ya maitshwaro ya tirwa mo go bona, ya tiragala—

(a)

ka rurifatso ya Komiti ya Boatlhodi; le

(b) ka tekatekanyo le go dirile mo pontshong le go sena tshogo, kgethololo le kgobatso.

(3) Kamogelo, madi a itshetsho le nngwe thuso ya maloko a boatlhodi a tswa mo Mading a Setshaba.

(4) Kamogelo, madi a itshetsho le nngwe thuso ya maloko a boatlhodi a kwa tshwanela go fokotswa fa ba ntse ba letshwari kana tlhatlhama setilo sa ofisi ya teng. KAROLO 3 Komiti Ya Boatlhodi 189 Tshimologo le pupego ya Komiti ya Boatlhodi

(1) Go na le Komiti ya Boatlhodi le le nang le—

(a)

Moatlhodi-Mogolo;

(c)

Tautona wa Baatlhodi wa Kgotla Kgolo;

(b) Mothusa wa Moatlhodi-Mogolo;

(d) moatlhodi a le mongwe o tlhophilweng ke baatlhodi ba Kgotla ya Molao Motheo, Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa, Kgotla Kgolo, Kgotla ya Babereki le Kgotla ya Tsamaiso;

(e)

(f)

Mmueledi-Mogolo;

mmakasetarata mogolo;

(g) modula-setulo wa Komiti ya Babereki;

(h) babereki ba ba bararo ba ba nna le dingwaga di le bosupa ba bereka ba le baemedi ba molao, ba tsentswe ke ba le phata, ga tlase ga Molao wa Palamente, o emelang baemedi ba molao mo Zimbabwe;

(i)

motlhatlhelela dithuto mogolo a le mongwe kgotsa o mongwe wa ba rutang ka ga tsa molao a emetse bontse tsa ba ba rutang ka molao ko di yunibesithing tsa Zimbabwe kgotsa, le fa go sena le phata la mofuta oo, a tlhophilwe ke Tautona;

(j)

motho a le mongwe o mo di ngwageng di le bosupa a bereka mo Zimbabwe a le modupa wa setshaba, le o tsentsweng ke ba le phata la bodupa, ga tlase ga molao wa Palamente, o emelang batho ba go nna jaalo; le

(k) motho a le mongwe wa dingwaga di le bosupa a bereka boitlhokomedi jwa morafe, a tlhophilwe ke Tautona.

(2) Moatlhodi-Mogolo kgotsa, fa a seo, Mothusa Moatlhodi-Mogolo o dula mo di kopanong tsa Komiti ya Boatlhodi, le mo bo seong jwa bone ka bobedi mo di kopanong maloko a leng teng a tlhopha o mongwe wa bone gu dula mokopanong.

(3) Maloko a Komiti ya Boatlhodi o go buiwang ka one mo di karolwaneng

(d), (h), (i), (j) le (k) wa temana (1) ba a tlhophiwa gangwe feela ga di ngwaga di le borataro. 190 Ditiro tsa Komiti ya Boatlhodi

(1) Komiti ya Boatlhodi e ka neela dikaela go Goromente maabapi le dikgang tse di amang le phata leo,kgotsa tsa boikatlego, gape Goromente o tshwanetse go tsaya sentle dikaela tse.

(2) Komiti ya Boatlhodi e tshwanetse go rotluetsa le go etelelapele kgololesego le boikarabelo jwa le phata le bo natla, mo go berekeng sentle, gape le na le maatla otlhe a go dira jaalo.

(3) Komiti ya Boatlhodi, ka bo dumedi jwa Tona wa tsa tolamo, o ka dira melao ya ditiro tse go buiwa ka tsone mo mophatong ee.

(4) Molao wa Palamente o ka tsenelela mo di tsamaisong tsa Komiti ya Boatlhodi le tshwaraganye le go dirisiwa, boitshwaro le diemo tsa batho ba ba hirilweng mo le kgotleng la Molao Motheo, le kgotla la ditsmaiso le makgotla a mangwe. 191 Go ikanyega le go nna pepene ne ga di thulaganyo tsa ba Komiti ya Boatlhodi Komiti ya Boatlhodi e tshwanetse go dira tiro ya yone ka go lekanetsa, tekatekano le go direla mo pontshong. KAROLO 4 Kakaretso 192 Molao o tshwanetse go dirisiwa Leano le le siametseng go laolwa ke dikgotla tsa Zimbabwe ke molao o neng o dirisiwa ka nako letsatsing la tshebetso, joale o fetotswe.

193 Boatlhodi jwa borukhutlhi ke dikgotla Dikgotla tse di latelang di tshwanetse go dira kana go neelwa tsheko ya borukhutlhi—

(a)

Kgotla ya Molao Motheo, Kgotla e Botlhokwatlhokwa, Kgotla Kgolo le dikgotla tsa mmakasetarata;

(b) kgotla kgotsa setlhopha se se dirang tsheko ga tlase ga molao wa go baakanya maitswaro, go fitlha boatlhodi ba tlhokagala go pateletsa paakanyo mo maitswarong a teng. KGAOLO 9 Tsamaiso Ya Morafe Le Boeteledipele 194 Makaedi a botlhokwa le boeteledipele jwa puso le tsamaiso ya morafe

(1) Tsamaiso ya morafe ga mabaka otlhe mogoromente, akaretsa dikgwebo dikgotla tsa lefatshe le dikgotla tse di laolwang ke goromente le dikgwebo ditshwanetse go laolwa ke goromente ka puso ya batho ka batho, makaedi le ketelelopele e e mabapi le popego ya Molao Motheo go akaretswa mo go latelang—

(a)

boleng jo bo kwa go dimo jwa tiro ya boitseanape ka maitseo a a mantle a tshwanetse go tlhatlosiwa gape a a tlhokomelwa;

(b) go bereka sentle le go somarela di diriswa go tshwanetse go tlhatloswa maemo;

(c)

tsamaiso tsa morafe di tshwanetse go nna le tlhabologo;

(d) bodiredilapuso botshwanetse go ipaankanyetsa go sena kgobelelo, ka fa molaong ka tekatenyo go sena kgathelelo;

(e)

tse di tlhokafalang tsa setshaba ditshwanetse go arabelwa ka bonako le di tsamaiso tsa morafe thotloetswa go tsaya karolo kana go nna le seabe go dira mokwalo wa tumalano ya tshireletso;

(f)

tsamaiso ya tsa morafe e tshwanetse gonna le maikarabelo ko Palamenteng le mo bathong;

(g) kgotla ya dikgwebo tsa goromente ka go lekana ditshwanetse go dirisana;

(h) go direla mo pontshong go tshwanetse go tsholetswa ka go tlamela morafe ka go neela kisto e siameng ka bonako;

(i)

go itshwara sentle, bokgoni jwa bookamedi tirisanyo le tlhabololo le bokgoni jwa batho ba ba rutegileng ka kitso e e tsepameng;

(j) bodiri, tsamaiso ya morafe bo tshwanetse go emela batho bangwe le ba bangwe ka karetso mefutafuta ya merafe ya lefatse la Zimbabwe;

(k) tiro, ikatiso le kgatelelopele di tshwanetse go dirwa ka bokgoni le ka nnete, bosiame le tekatekano ya banna le basadi go akaretsa batho ba banang le bogole; gape Lefatshe le tshwanetse go tsaya dikgato dingwe go akaretsa molao motheo le go tsholetsa maemo, makaedi le matshegediwa.

(2) Tlhopho ya motho o berekelang mo di ofising tsa goromente, go akaretsa maphatla, dikgotla a ga goromente, le di kgwebo tse di laolwang ke puso le tse dingwe tse dibotlhokwa ditshwanetse go dirwa ka pele ka bokgoni.

195 Papadi tsa Lefatse tse dilaolwang ke goromente

(1) Ditlhopha le tsa papadi dingwe tse dilaolwang ke puso ditshwanetse go tlaleletsa go obamela tsamaiso tse di beilweng mo mphato 194(1), etelela mokgwa wa go bereka gore go tsweledise kana go tlhokomela, go na teng ga dipapadi go (sala morago) etelelwa ba ka selekanyo sa boleng mo go siametseng tsa goromente.

(2) Dikompone le tsa papadi se se buiwang ke temana (1) e tshwanetse go supa tshimololo le kgaisano mo go tsamaisang theko. 196 Maikarabelo a badirelapuso le tsamaiso ya boeteledipele

(1) boitseanape/(maatla) jo boneelwang badirelapuso, ke tshepo a tshwanetse go diriswa jana—

(a)

maitlamo a sa fetogeng ka maikaelelo a a popego ya Molao Motheo;

(b) go supegetsa le go tlotla morafe kgotsa setshaba e e bo be be iketleetso go direla go sena go taolo ya batho;

(c)

go tsholetsa tsepo le go tlotla setshaba mo badirelapuso.

(2) Badirelapuso ba tshwanetse go ikokobetsa mo bathong botlhe go direla go kgatholosa tlhoka kutlwisisano fa gare ga di kgatlhego tsa bone, semmuso le go sa tlhopha mo go bontsang lenyatso la ofisi ya bone.

(3) Boiteledipele jwa badirelapuso bo tshwanetse go latela tsamaiso jwa boeleledipele jo bo latelang—

(a)

bonnete le go sa thlopha mo tswetsong;

(c)

nna le boikarabelo mo morafeng ka tshwetso le kgatho; le

(b) boammaruri mo tirong ya setshaba;

(d) botho le maitseo le maikemisetso go direla semmorafe 197 Mabaka a fiwang di tlhogo tsa goromente tse di laolwang maphata a goromente Molao wa Palamente o ka fa selekanyo sa sebaka a fiwang baookamedi kgotsa ditlhogo tse di laolwang dingwe, dikgwebo le ditiro tsa setshaba tse di laolwang go thelele ke goromente. 198 Molao motheo o gatelelang Kgaolo 9 Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go tlamela tshwetso ya gatelela dino tsa Kgaolo ee, go akaretsa tshwetso—

(a)

go tlhoka gore baookamedi ba setshaba go dira pasalatso ya ditlhogo tsa bone ka tlwaelo;

(b) go godisa melao le maitshwaro le go lemogiwa ke ba bodirelapuso;

(c) ya go kgetha boleng jwa puso e siame go lemogwa ke maphata a ga goromente le tse dingwe dikgwebo tse di laolwang ke Lefatshe gotlhelele;

(d) go paankanyetsa batho ba maitseo a tsamayelaneng le Kgaolo le boitshwaro jo bo buiwang ke temana (b).

KGAOLO 10 Bodirela Puso 199 Bodirela puso

(1) Gona le bodiredi puso bole bongwe fela jo bo nnang le maikarabelo a a botsamisi jwa ketelelo pele lefatseng la Zimbabwe.

(2) Bodiredi puso bonale batho baba berekelang lefatse babalatelang—

(a)

Maloko atshireletso sechaba leba direlang puso basele bakanang basupiwa;

(b) Baatlodi, mmakaseterata, lebatho ba etelang bokopano jwa lekgotla tse di beiloeng ke kgatto ya palamente;

(c)

Maloko a a lekgotlha la basekaseki a a supilweng ke popego ya molao motheo;

(d) Badiredi ba palamente; le

(e)

Babereki babangwe babamo ofising kgotsa mopako moseemong sa popego ya molao motheo kotsa bodiredi jwa palamente go sa bopa bongwe jwa badirela puso.

(3) Molao wa Palamente o tshwanetse go baankanyetsa tsa lekgotla, tholaganyo, bookamedi, molawana botho le go obamela (203) mabaka a tiro a maloko a Badirelapuso. 200 Goitshwara ga maloko a direlang puso

(1) Maloko abodirela pusoatshwanetse go dira mabapi le popego ya molao motheo le molao.

(2) Ga go dumelwi gore maloko a badiredi puso gore balatele.

(3) Badirela puso a ba tshwanela go dirisa mo go latelang—

(a)

Gotsaya kgato, ledikgatlego mo phathing yasepolotiki;

(c)

A a tshwanela gona le kgethololo ledikgatlhego tsa phati yase polotiki; kana

(b) Go gothusa gotsweledisa dikgatlego tsa phati yase polotiki;

(d) Goroba kotsa gosalatela ditshwanelo lekhololo yabatho.

(4) Badiredi puso ga batshwanela goitshwaragana letsa sepolotiki.

(5) Kgato ya palamente ittshwanetse go dira ditlamelo thomamiso tsa go sa tsaya letlhakore sepoliliki ya bodiredi puso. 201 Tona ya bodirela puso Tau tona otshwanetse go tlhopha tona elebaganeng le go direla puso. 202 Madirelo le thamo tsa badirela puso ba kgotla la ba sekaseki

(1) Gona lle lekgotla laba diredi puso labatho baba latelang—

(a)

Modulasetilo lemo thusa dulasetilo; le

(b) Maloko amabedi goa momalokong ale matthlano asele, babathlophiwang ke Tau tona.

(2) Lekgotla laba diredi puso le tshwanetse go tlhophiwa kakitso, le maitemogelo tsamaiso le bookamedi kgotsa ditlamelo tsa bodiredi ba morafe. 203 Tiro ya badirela puso

(1) Lekgotla laba direla puso letshwanetse godira ditiro tselatelang—

(a)

Gothlopha batho baba nang le boitsanapelebo kgone jwa godira ditiro mo bodireleng puso;

(b) Bonthabongwe jwa kgang karolo 65(5) ya go bakanya itshwanetsi go obamela taolo lemabaka aa amang bodiredi go akaretsa kamogelo,madia tshetso le dituelo tsa maloko abo direla puso;

(c)

Godirisa taolo lebokgone jo bo mabapi lemaloko a adirelang puso;

(e)

Go i tsenya mo tirisanong le godira kanatla lego tsaya kgato go tlhomamisiwa, le tshupegetso bontla moteng le go sa papamala le boitekanelo jwa badiredi puso;

(f)

Gotlhomamisa batirela puso go tiragatsa ditlhamego kabonatla go sena khobelelo;

(d) Gotlhotlhomisa hutsagalo lego naya kalafi ya bo hutsana mogo lebaganeng labadiredi puso mabapi lefa molaong (semmuso) kgotsa tlolo;

(g) Go fa kgakololo Tau Tona ledi tona dimgwe ytse do amang bodiredi puso;

(h) Gotsholetsa maemo a bodiredi puso makaedi le molao wa maitsholo go supegetsa molao motheo; le

(i)

Godirisa le tirisanyo tse seele tse dinelang pateletso mole kgotleng la basekaseki go bapa le molao motheo kgotsa kgato ya palamente.

(2) Lekgotla labasekaseki letumelanyo ya tona ye lebaganeng lebo direla puso nka dira melawana gaga maikaelelo kgotsa matlamo itsise ditiragalo.

(3) Botirela puso botshwanetsegodirisa tiriso ka tshwanelo ya mokwalo watumalano ya tshireletso ka maikalelo. taeloefe, tona yabodiredi puso.

(4) Ga gobakanywa dituelo, madi a a itshetso le dituelo tse seele tse badiredi puso, le kgotla la basekaseki letshwanetse go dira ka tumelano ya Tau tona e neelwe ktona elebaganeng le tsa madi kwa morago ga therisano le Tona ennang le maikarabelo mo ba direding puso. 204 Baemedi le ba ba botlhokwa mo Zimbabwe Tau tona nka tlhoma motho/batho gonna baemei kgotsa baemedi baba botlhokwa ba lefatse la Zimbabwe ko mafatseng a seele kgotsa baba lttleletswing makgotleng ama fatshefatshe nako ngwe le ngwe. 205 Bakwaledi ba sennelaruri

(1) Bakwaledi ba sennelaruri balephatla la ga goromente bafiwa/tlhophiwa ke Tao tona gosena/morago ga therisano, puisano le lekgotla labasekaseki.

(2) Mokwaledi wa sennelaruri ofiwa dingwaga tse tlhanu mommanong kgotsa dikanna tsa okelediwa gangwe feela gape bakgona go boeletswa golebeletswe bokgoni. Tiragatso lepelegi.

KGAOLO 11 Ba Tshireletso KAROLO 1 Dino Fela 206 Tshireletso setshaba

(1) Maikaelelo a tshereletso setshaba gaga Zimbabwe kego tshwanela go bontsa, supa,tsaya tshwetso ya batho mo Zimbabwe go tshela kago lekana lemo khololesegong /kagiso le kutlwano, go i pusa go sena letshogo gona le katlego.

(2) Maikaelelo a a tshireletso ga Zimbabwe atshwanetse ga na abolokesegile ka go dumelana (adumelanang le molao motheo).

(3) Pabalelo ya ntlha yabodiredi jwa tshireletso-itshwanetse golelekela/go tswelela katlotlo ethata—

(a)

ditshwanelo tse dibotlhokwa, khololo puso yabatho ka batho ma ekaedi tsamaiso e e karetsweng mo molaong motheo; le

(b) le taolo molao. 207 Bodiredi jwa tshireletso

(1) Bodiredi jwa itshereletso jwa Zimbabwe boning le—

(a) setlhopha samasole;

(b) setlhopha sama podisi;

(c) setlhopha saba tsaya dikitso;

(d) bakgolegelo leba tse dingwe; le

(e) le tse disupilweng ke kgato ya palamente.

(2) Ba bodiredi jwa itshireletso batshwnetse go obamela bolaodi jwa popego ya molao motheo Tau Tona, le kuduthamaga ya puso batshwanetsego obamela palamente.

(3) Maloko abodiredi jwa tshireletso ba tshwanetse go akanyetsa mefutafuta ya merafe ya Zimbabwe. 208 Maitshwaro a maloko a bodiredi jwa tshireletso ya setshaba

(1) Maloko abadiredi jwa itshereletso a tshwanetse go tsaya kgato ka fa tsamaisong ya popego ya molao motheo.

(2) Epe bodiredi jwa tshireletso lefa ele bongwe ba maloko nka dirisa gaga bone motirong—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

batsee kgato ka mothale; katlego e e tla thusang go tsweledisa bongwe ga sepolotiki le modi; kgobelelo yadi katlego tse mo molaong tsa bongwa go sepolotiki; kana goroba maikaelelo aa botlhokwa kgotsa kgololo motho mangwe.

(3) Maloko a a bodiredi jwa tshireletso ga a a tshwanela go nna matlhagatlhaga kgotsa morwadi ofisi yasepolotiki kgotsa lekgotla.

(4) Ba bereki ba tshiretso a ba tshwanela go dira engwe tiro kana go nna le seabe mo dikgotleng tsa morafe kwantle fa gona le tshoganetso setshaba.

209 Khansele ya tshireletso setshaba

(1) Gona le khansele ya itshireletso setshaba e e nang le Tautona ja ka modulasetilo le mothusa tautona le ditona le malokok a a direlang tshireletso le batho be sele ja ka nka laolwang ke Molao wa Palamente.

(2) Tiro ya khansele ya itshireletso—

(a)

go tlotlafatsa melawana ta tshireletso;

(b) go itsise le go gakolola Tautona ka dingwe tse di amanyang tshereletso setshaba; ne

(c)

go itshidila nngwe le nngwe tiro elayang kgatoya palamente.

(3) Baeteledipele bat shireletso setshaba batshwanetse go tlamela khansili ya tshreletso setshaba ka ipego mo seeming sa tshireletso setshaba mo lefatseng la Zimbabwe ja ka khansil nka tlhokang. 210 Mekgwa wa ngongorego Kgato ya palamente itshwanetse go tlamela dikarolo tse kemetseng tse dirilweng ka natla gag a kamogelo dithothomiso tsa ngongorego tsa maloko a se morafe ka nna. Thloka maitshwaro mo ntheng tsa maloko a a bodirela tshireletso setshaba le go fa kalafi tshenyo kgotsa kgobalo nngwe e e dirilweng ke ba ba tlhokang maitshwaro. KAROLO 2 Setlhopha Sa Masole 211 Masole

(1) Setlhopha sa Masole a a Zimbabwe a nna le masole asefofane le bodiredi jo bongwe e nkang supang ka fa tlase kwa kgatho ya palamente.

(2) Setlhopha sa Masole ke sone fela se se letlelesega ka fa molaong mo Zimbabwe.

(3) Setlhopha sa Masole setshwanetse go tlotla mo go bo tlhokwa ditshwanelo le kgololo tsa batho botlhe ba setshaba go rata lefatse la bone/gabone, boitseanape le mmereki yo o mmotlana jaka gosupilwe mo molaong motheo.

(4) Setlhopha sa Masole itshireletso setshaba setshwanetse go tsweledisa maitseo kotsa botho.

(5) Kgatho ya palamente itshwanetse go ipaakantsa lephata kgotsa le kgotla thulaganyo bo okamedi,molawana le tlhatloso lepuseltso kwatlase ga batho bamaemo le maloko a a seele atshwanetseng go obamela karolo 218 ya bodiredi jwa maloko a a tshireletso setshaba. 212 Tiro ya Masole Tiro ya Setlhopha sa Masole ke go tshireletsa lefatse la Zimbabwe, batho tshireletshego ga setshaba. Dikgatlhego le dikgaolo ka boammaaruri kgotsa go tsweledisa tshwetso tumelo mo molaong motheong. 213 Mafelo a tiro ya Sesole

(1) Kobamelo molao motheo wa Zimbabwe Tautona fela ja ka moeteledipele wa setlhopha samasole ona le matla a go—

(a) go dumelela tsamaiso ya masole a a itshereletso setshaba kgotsa;

(b) o na le bokgone jwa go laola le go dirisiwa ga masole atshereletso setshaba.

(2) Ka boitseanape jwa Tautona setlhopha samasole nka rungwa gongwe le gongwe mo fatseng la Zimbabwe—

(a) go tshireletsa Zimbabwe;

(b) go tshegetsa ma podisi le go tsweledisa kagiso; kana

(c) go tshegetsa ma podisi le batho baba seng masole kanako ya tshoganetso kgotsa matlhotlhapelo.

(3) Katumelo le bokgone jwa ga Tautona, masole a a setshaba nka tswela kontle ga le fatse la Zimbabwe—

(a)

ka mokgwa wa gobereka, kagiso le kagisano kanna ya dirwa ka tumalano ya le kgotlha la Kopanelanyo ditshaba kgotsa mafatsefatse kgotsa ga dikgaolo fa Zimbabwe enang le boloko jo botletseng;

(b) go sireletsa di kgaolo le boammaaruri jwa mafatse a a seele;

(c) ka tigelo le maitlamo a a mafatsefatse; kana

(d) tshereletso le di katlego tsa setshaba sa Zimbabwe.

(4) Bobedi mo borarong jwa bontsi ba palo yotlhe yamaloko a a palamente go kopanya le kgotla ya di tona kgotsa magosi kgobokano setshaba, palamente nka tsaya tshwetso gore gotswa gamasole kwantle ga lefatse la Zimbabwe gotshwanetse.

(5) Palaente ga esetse etseri tshwetso gore masole atla tswela kwantle ga Zimbabwe tautona otshwanetse gore atse kgatho go ntsha setlhopha samasole atlhalusa gape a athomamisa og tlhokafalang lebaba direlang tshereletso le didirisiwa mo le fatseng la Zimbabwe. 214 Maikarabelo a sepolotiki mo mmerekong wa setlhopha sa Masole Fa setlhopha sa masole siya mmerekong—

(a)

go thusa lego naya tlamelo ga morafeng mo Zimbabwe kgotsa; kana

(b) kwantle ga Zimbabwe;

Tautona otshwanetse go bulelela palamente, kabofefo leka tshwanelo kabotlalo aneela mabaka a go dira jalo gape—

(i) Fa masole atla direlang mo lefatseng la Zimbabwe;

(ii) kwa masole ayang go bereka kwantle ga Zimbabwe le lefatse ga batla berelelang. 215 Tona o na le maikarabelo mo Setlhopheng sa Masole Tautona otshwanetse go tlhopha kgotsa go thama tona etla nag le maikarabelo fa Setlhopheng sa Masole. 216 Taolo ka Masole a tshireletso

(1) Molao wa Palamente e ka tlamela go re—

(a)

setlhopha sa Masole a a itshereletso go etelelwapele kwatlase gwa taolo enngwe fela; kana

(b) bodiredi bo nngwe ja masole a a tshereletso kgotsa tsepedi kgotsa ka bontsi, kakpanelo boka nna le ba eteledipele ba a kgaoganeng.

(2) moeteledipele mongwe le mongwe wa setlhopha samasole atshereletso, le mongwe moeteledipele wa bodiredi jwa masole, utlhophiwa kgotsa othamiwa ke Tautona mo rago ga terisao le tona enang le boikarabelo jwa masole a a itshereletso.

(3) Baeteledipele ba masole atshereletso le bodiredi jwa boeteledipele jwa masole bo tlhophiwa kgotsa thamiwa ga sebaka se se kwatlase ga dingwaga tse tlhano, gape motho ga a tshwanela dirale nngwe ya di ofisi tseo go fitla mabaka amabedi.

(4) motho yo o dirileng kgotsa yo berekileng ja ka moeteledipele wa bo diredi jwa masole a a tshereletso nka nna a thamiwa jaka moeteledipele ga masoleng a tshereletso, mme motho yo o diretseng a ka moeteledipele wa sesole sa tshereletso ga a kake a thamiwa jaka moeteledipele wa bodiredi ba sesole sa itshereletso kgotsa goa ko taolong nngwe ya bodiredi tshereletse

(5) Moeteledipele mongwe le mongwe le moeteledipele wa bodiredi jwa setlhopha samasole otshwanwetse go itshidila taolo ya gagwe ka fa tsamaisong mokwalo wa tumalano ya tshereletso ka taelo e e omakilweng ke tona enang le boikarabelo jwa setlhopha samasole adirang kwa tlase ga bolaodi jwa ga Tautona. 217 Tshimolodiso le thamo ya le kgotla la basekseki ba se Sole

(1) Gona le lekgotlha la basekaseki la setlhopha samasole atshereletso anang le modula setilo yo o tshwanwtseng gonna modula setilo wa badiredi bale kgotla la basekaseki, le lekabo yamaloko amabedi le le ama rataro ka bointsi le maloko asele a tlhophilweng ke tautona.

(2) Maloko ale kgotla labasekaseki la masole atshereletso atshwanetse go tlhophiwa ka kitso ya bone maitemogelo mo le Tsamaisong kgotsa ketelelopele, bookamedi godira ka tsa masole, boiteanape jwabone kgotsa botshwanedi jwa go dira letsa tiro.

(a)

kabo kgutswane batho bawo gobatshwanela gore bane batho babadirileng mo sesoleng jaka maloko asetlhopa samasole;

(b) gabonyenyane mongwe wabone otshwanetse gore one atshwara maemo a a magolwane mo masoleng a tshereletso ga sebaka kgotsa mabaka aselekanyo sa dingwaga tse tlhano kabonyenyane. 218 Tiro ya Lekgotla la Sesole

(1) Lekgotla laba sekaseki la Setlhopha sa Masole senang le ditiro tse latelang—

(a)

go hira batho ba ban ale boitseanape le bokgoni tiro maemo mo Setlhopheng sa Masole;

(b) go baakanya lego laola mabaka a a amang tiro, go akaretsa tuelo madi a a itshetso le go a a mogela thuso tsa maloko a a setlhopha samasole;

(c) go tlhomamisa, go akaretsa ka boitekanelo le botsamaisi jo bontle ba setlhopha samasole le tlamelo e e kwagodimo le godira ka natla;

(d) go tlhomamisa gore maloko a a setlhopha samasole se obamela karolo 208;

(e) go tlhabolola kutlwano le kutlwisisano fagare ga masole le batho baba seeng masole;

(f) go gakolola Tautona le Tona ka dingwe tse di amanyang le setlhopha samasole; kana

(g) go itshidila tiro nngwe, go buisana kgotsa go neela kgotsa go pateletsa mo le kgotleng labasekseki gobapa le molao motheo kgotsa Kgato ya palamente.

(2) Le kgotla labasekaseki latirelo les tlhopha samasole atshereletso ka tumalano ya tona enang le boikarabelo ga setlhopheng samasole etshereletso nka dira melawana gotlhelele kgotsa maitlamo go supegediwa bontlhabongwe (1).

(3) Ga go baakangwa di tuelo kgotsa kamogelo, madi a a itshetso le di tuelo tsa maloko a a setlhopha samasole a a tshereletso, la kgotla labasekeseki le tirelo ya setlhopha samasole a a tshereletse atshwanetse go dira ka tumelelo ya ga Tautona go umakilwe puelelelo ya Tona enang le boikarabelo ba tsa madi morago ga terisano le tona enang le maikarabelo ga masole a tshereletso setshaba. KAROLO 3 Bodiredi Jwa Sepodisi 219 Bodiredi jwa Sepodisi

(1) Gona lebo diredi jwa mapodisi jo bo naleng boikarabelo—

(a)

go batlisisa, go tlhomamisa;

(c)

go sereletsa le tshereletso matshelo a batho le dithoto;

(e)

Go tshweledisa molao motheo oo le go gatelela molao go sena letshogo.

(b) tshereletso moteng ga Zimbabwe;

(d) go tlhokomela molao le taela; me

(2) Bodiredi jwa bopodisi botshwanetse go tirisa itshidilo mo Tirisanyong le—

(a)

ntlha babodiredi jwa botlhale eng ka simolodisa ke molao;

(c)

dikgaolo le mafatsefatse tse dibopilweng go lwantsa Borukuti

(b) mongwe oo nka simolodisa ka molao kwa maikaelelo a go batlisa; le

(3) bodiredi jwa sepodisi gab a tshwanela nna sepe ga Setshaba moanelwa kgotsa ka popego ,gorata lefatshe la gagabo ,ane leboitseanape le le nna mmereki yoo Nang le maikarabelo mo bathong jaka tshimolodi ya Molao motheo.

(4) Kgatho ya palamente itshwanetse go tlamela tsa le Kgotla, thulaganyo bookamedi, molawana lebo Boitshwaro jo bo siameng le tlhatloso le go diga maemo le maloko a a seele le moono wa kang 223 mabaaka a amang tiro a a bodiredi jwa mapodisi. 220 Tona wa Sepodisi Tautona otshwanetse go tlhopha Tona e e tla nang le Boikarabelo mo bo direding jwa mapodisi. 221 Ramapodisi

(1) Bodiredi jwa mapodisi bo kwatlase ga taolo ya Mogolwane yo tlhophilweng wa mapodisi ya thamilweng ke Tautona morago ga puisano le Tona yase podisi enang le boikarabelo.

(2) Mogolwane yo mogolo yo otlhophilweng ga sebaka dingwaga tse tlhano tse nka ntshafadiwa gangwe fela.

(3) Motho yo o diretseng jaka Mogolwane wa Mapodisi yo tlhophilweng ke lekgotla ga ankake a a tlhophiwa go etlelelapele mo bodireding jwa tshireletso.

(4) Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng ke le kgotla otshwanetse Go itshidila taolo ya gagwe ka tshwanelo le mokwalo wa watumalano ya tshireletso yo o kwadilweng taelo go umakilwe ke Tona enang le boikarabelo ga mapodisi a tsaya kgato kwa tlase ga taolo ya ga Tau tona.

222 Tshimologo le Tlhamo ya Lekgotla la Mapodisi

(1) Gona le lekgotla labasekaseki ba sepodisi enang le modulasetilo yo tshwanetseng gonna modulasetilo wa bodirelapuso gape lemaloko ka bonnyennyane jwa maloko ale mabedi le bontsi jwa maloko a le marataro baba tlhophilweng ke Tautona.

(2) Maloko a a lekgotla la basekaseki la badirela sepodisi atshwanetse go tlhophiwa ka kitso kgotsa le maitemogelo mo go tswelediseng molo le taelo, tsamaiso kgotsa boitseanape ja bone kgotsa go sa papamala ga matshwanedi ga maemo—

(a)

le gale sephatlo sa batho ba setshwanetse baba seng maloko abodiredi jwa sepodisi pele;

(b) le gale mongwe wabone otshwanetse gore an motho o neng anle maemo amagolwane mo bodireding jwa sepodisi mongwe kgotsa gamabaka amaantsi ga selekanyo sa ngwaga tse tlhano. 223 Tiro ya Lekgotla la Mapodisi

(1) Lekgotla la Mapodisi bona le ditiro tse latelang—

(a)

go hira batho baba nale boitseanape lebo kgoni go tshwara maemo mo bo direla sepodisi;

(b) go baakanya le go laola mabaka a amang tiro go akaretsa kamogelo ,madi a tshetso, le dituelo tse dingwe tsa maloko a a bodiredi sepodisi;

(c)

go tlhomamisa boitekanelo le tsamaiso esiameng jwa badiredi sepodisi le go tlhokomela le seemo se se kwagodimo sa bonatla;

(d) go tlhomamisa gore maloko a a bodiredi sepodisi obamela karolo 208;

(e)

go gatelela kutlwanole kutlisisano ga fare bodidi sepodisi le batho fela;

(f)

go gakolola Tautona le tona enang le boikarabelo ka dingwe amanyang bodiredi jwa sepodisi; me

(g) go itshidiladitiro dingwe conferred anf imposed mo molaong motheo o o kgotsa Kgato ya palamente.

(2) Le kgotla laba sekaseki la bodiredi mapodisi katumelelo ya tona enang le boikarabelo jwa bodiredi jwa mapodisi nka dira melawana go supegetsa mo bontlhabongwe (1).

(3) Mo go baakanyeng di tuelo, madi atshwetso le di thuso dingwetsa se podisi lekgotla laba sekaseki la bodiredi jwa sepodisi botshwanetse go dira ka tumalano ya ga Tautona ka puelelo ga tona enang le boikarabelo tsa madi gape gosina go buisana le tona enang le boikarabelo jwa bodiredi ba sepodisi. KAROLO 4 Setlhopha Sa Botlhali 224 Tshimologo ya Setlhopha sa Botlhali

(1) Bongwe jwa bodiredi botlhale ja goromente boseele ga karolo ya a ya setlhopha sa tshereletso sa masole kgotsa sa bodiredi jwa mapodisi atshwanetse go simolodisa ka mabaka a a molao le taelo ga Tautona kgotsa khuduthamaga ya puso kgotsa taela.

(2) Bongwe jwa bodiredi botlhale jwa goromente e e tshwanetse go sa amana le tsa sepolotiki, ga setshaba gorata le fatshe, boitseanape le babereki baba motlana le bolaodi jwa batho jaka go simolodiwa ga molao motheo o o. 225 Tona wa Setlhopha sa Botlhali Tautona oo tshwanetse go tlhopha Tona enang le boikarabelo jwa bodiredi botlhali ba setshaba. 226 Taolo kgotsa laolo tsa bodiredi jwa botlhale ba setshaba

(1) Bodiredi jwa botlhale ga setshaba botshwanetse go nna kwatlase ga taolo ya Motsaisi o o mogolwane wa bodiredi ba bo tlhale yo o tshwanetseng go thamiwa ke Tautona ga sebaka sa dingwaga tse tlhano tse di ka ntshafadiwa gangwe fela.

(2) Motsaisi kgotsa Molaodi omogolwane wa bodiredi Botlhale otshwanetse go dirisa taolo le laolo ka fa tshwanelong le mokwalo wa tumalao ya tshereletso taolo go umakilwe Tona e enang le maikarabelo le bodiredi ja bothlale jwa setshaba go dirwa kwa tlase ga taolo ya ga Tautona.

(3) Motho yo one a bereka jaka Motsamaisi Omogolwane ko bodireding jwa botlhale ga akake a tlhophiwa go nna molaodi wa bodiredi ba tshereletso.

KAROLO 5 Kgolegelo Le Bodiredi Jwa Paakanyo 227 Kgolegelo le Bodiredi jwa Paakanyo le ditiro tsa yo one

(1) Go nna le Kgolelgelo le Bodiredi jwa Paakanyo jo bo nang le maikarabelo a go—

(a)

Pabalelo ya morafe gotsweng serukuting ka incarceration le go paakanyo tlaloganyo ya tsa batlhatlhediwa batho ba ba tlhatlhetswing la ba bangwe bal letlelesegang ka fa molaong ba batlhokang go tlhatlhelwa le go buseletswa gabone mo morafeng; le

(b) tsamaiso ya a kgolegelo le ditlamelo tsa paakanyo.

(2) Kgolegelo le bodiredi jwa paakanyo ga ba tshwanela go tsaya kgatho epe mosepolotikini semorafe go rata lefatse labone, boitseanape le le godira ka molao wa batho fela jaka simolodi ya molao motheo.

(3) Kgatho ya Palamente e tshwanetse tlamela mo kgaolo thulaganyo, molawana, maitseo le go tsholetsa maemole go diga maemo ga baokamedi le maloko a seele go tswang karolo 231 mabaka a amang go direla maloko bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le paakanyo. 228 Tona e enang le maikarabelo mobodireding jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo Tautona oo tshwanetse go tlhopha Tona e enang le maikarabelo lebo diredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo.

229 Mogolwane yo o thlophilweng wa bo diredi jwa Kgolegelo le paakanyo

(1) Bodired jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo bo kwa tlase go Mogolwane yo tlhophilweng wa Bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo yo otlhophiwang ke Tautona morago ga therisano le Tona enang le maikarabelo momBodireding Jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo.

(2) Mogolwane yo ottlhophilweng wa Bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo o o tlhophiwa ga sebaka tsa dingwaga tse tlhano eke nang ya ntshafadiwa gangwe fela.

(3) Motho yo o diretseng Bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo jaka Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng wa Bodiredi jwa Kgollegelo le Paakanyo nka nna a thamiwa/ tlhophiwa go laola mo bodireding ja tshereletso.

(4) Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng mo bodireding jwa Kgolegelo le Paankanyo otshwanetse go go itshidila taolo ya a gagwe ka fa tshwanelong ya mokwalo wa tumalano yatshereletso go umakilwe ke Tona enang le maikarabelo fa bodireding jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo atsays kgato le bolaodi jwa Tautona. 230 Tshimologo le thamo ya Bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paankanyo

(1) Gona le lekgotla la Basekaseki ja bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo e dirwang ke modulasetilo yo otshwanetseng go nna modulasetilo wa wa lekgotla labasekaseki la bodirelapuso le bonyenyane ja maloko ale mabedi go ya bontsing ja maloko a le marataro ka bointsi batlhophilweng ke Tautona.

(2) Maloko a a lekgotla la ba sekaseki la bodiredi jwa Kgogelelo le Paakanyo a tshwanetsi go tlhophiwa ka kitso ya kgotsa ka tsamaiso, bookamedi tshereletso affair kgotsa ga boitseanape kgotsa matshwanedi a a go thamiwa le—

(a)

le gale sephatlo sa batho ba setshwanetse baba seng maloko abodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo pele;

(b) le gale mongwe wabone otshwanetse gore an motho o neng anle maemo amagolwane mo bodireding jwa Kgolelgelo le Paakanyo ngwe kgotsa gamabaka amaantsi ga selekanyo sa ngwaga tse tlhano. 231 Tiro ya lekgotla la ba sekaseki ba bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le Paakanyo

(1) Le kgotla la basekaseki jwa Bodiredi ja Kgolegelo le Paakanyo e nale ditiro tse latelang—

(a)

go hira batho ba ban ale boitseanpe le bo kgoni gotshware maemo a a tiro mo bodireding jwa Kgolegelo le Paakanyo;

(b) go baakanya le go laola mabaka a amang tiro go akaretsa kamogelo, madi a tshetso, le dituelo tse dingwe tsa maloko a a bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le paakanyo;

(c)

go tlhomamisa boitekanelo le tsamaiso esiameng jwa badiredi jwa kgolegelo le paakanyo le go tlhokomela le seemo se se kwagodimo sa bonatla;

(d) go tlhomamisa gore maloko a a bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le paankanyo le go obamela karolo 208;

(e)

go gatelela kutlwanole kutlisisano ga fare ga bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le paankanyo le batho fela;

(f)

2013

go gakolola Tautona le tona enang le boikarabelo ka dingwe amanyang bodiredi jwa kgolegelo le paakanyo;

(g) go itshidiladitiro dingwe conferred and imposed mo molaong motheo o o kgotsa Kgato ya palamente.

(2) Le kgotla laba sekaseki la bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo le paakanyo katumelelo ya tona enang le boikarabelo jwa bodiredi jwa Kgolegelo nka dira melawana go supegetsa mo bontlhabongwe (1).

(3) Mo go baakanyeng di tuelo, madi atshwetso le di thuso dingwetsa se podisi lekgotla laba sekaseki la bodiredi jwa sepodisi botshwanetse go dira ka tumalano ya ga Tautona ka puelelo ga tona enang le boikarabelo tsa madi gape gosina go buisana le tona enang le boikarabelo jwa bodiredi ba kgolegelo le paakanyo.

KGAOLO 12 Makgotla A A Basekseki A Kemetseng A Puso Yabatho Ka Batho KAROLO 1 Go Sa Papamala 232 Dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse di ikemetseng Dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse di kemetseng tse di latelang—

(a)

Lekgotla la ba sekaseki la botlhophi mo Zimbabwe;

(c)

Lekgotla la b asekaseki la bong mo Zimbabwe;

(e)

Lekgotla la basekaseki la kagiso le kagisano.

(b) Lekgotla la ba sekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa batho mo Zimbabwe;

(d) Lekgotla la basekaseki latsamaiso di kgang mo Zimbabwe; le

233 Maikaelelo a a dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse kemetseng Dikgotla tsa basekaseki tse di kemetseng di nna le maikaelelo motlaleletsang tseo go umakilwe ka bongwe—

(a)

go tshegetsa le go bopa ditshwanelo tsa setho le puso ya batho ka batho;

(c)

go tlatlosa maemo go molaong motheo;

(e)

go babalela le go obamela makaedi le melao ya maitsholo ya lefatse le dikgaolo le di kgwebo tsa goromente le di kgwebo tea di laolwang ke goromente; me

(b) go sereletsa magatse a a pusang le dikatlego tsa batho;

(d) go tlatlosa bosiame le go nna le maikarabelo mo dikgwebong tsa morafe;

(f)

go tlhomamisa gore gone le kalafi mo di kgobelelong.

234 Bodiredi jwa le kgotla labasekaseki jo bo ekemetseng Le kgotla labasekaseki le ekemetseng la nna le bokgoni jwa go hira babereki le sediri sa molao go laola mabaka a a bodiredi.

235 Lekgotla labasekaseki le ekemetseng

(1) Le kgotla labasekaseki le ekemetseng—

(a)

le nna le puso ya batho ka batho le go sa dira ka kaelo le ka taolo ya mongwe;

(b) tshwanetse go dira ka fa tsamaisong le molao motheo oo; le

(c)

tshwanetse go dira di tiro tsa bone go sena letshogo,go rata mongwe kgotsa kgethololo ntswa banna le boikarabelo moo palamente ka gobereka sentle go tshupegediwa mo di tirong tsa bonne.

(2) Goromente le di kgaolo tsotlhe le di kgwebo tsa goromente ka go lekanya fag are ga molao lle dilekanyo tse seele di tshwanetse go thusa le kgotla labasekaseki le le ekemetseng le tshwanetse go sereletsa boipuso ka go sa tlhopha, buammaaruri le go dira ka natla.

(3) Ga gona mongwe oo nka tsenelelang ka di tiro le go bereka sentle ga lekgotla laba sekaseki le ekemetseng. 236 Maloko a a basekaseki ba e kemetseng ba ba seng basepolotiki

(1) Maloko a a basekaseki ba ekemetseng ga a la tshwanela gore mo direng ditiro tsa bone ba—

(a) tseye kgato ka mokgwa wa sepolotiki;

(b) supegetse di katlego jwa sepolotiki sepe kgotsa modi;

(c) kgethololo ka ga molaong di katlego tsa phathi yasepolotiki epe kgotsa; le

(d) go roba di tshwanelo le kgololo tsa batho tse di botlhokwa.

(2) Batho ba baleng maloko a a sepolotiki kgotsa lekgotlha mo go tlameng ga bone mo bodireding jwa basekaseki jo boikemetseng tshwanetse go gore a a leese gona leloko go sena tiego mme mo tiragalong ngwe mo teng ga malatsi a lemasome a a mararo ga asena go tlamiwa.

(3) Ga leloko labasekaseki le ekemetseng—

(a) a a nna leeloko la phathi ya sepolotiki kgotsa lekgotlha kgotsa; kana

(b) ga nne atsere mmano jaka leloko la phathi ya sepolotiki kgotsa lekgotla mog tlhophiwang ga gagwe ga le kgotlleng la basekseki aa palelwa ke go tugela boloko jo mo teng ga malatsi a a le masome a a mararo oo emissiwa gone foo gona leloko lale kgotla labasekaseki. 237 Tlhopho le tloso go tsweng mo ofising ya boloko babasekeseki jo boi kemetseng

(1) Ga maikaelelo go tlhophiwa ga motho go na moemedi go nna mo lekgotleng labasekseki, Komoti ya melao e e eming le tlhomaganyo itshwanetse—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

go bapatswe bonno; go laletsa morefe go tlhopha; etelela potsolotso morafeng mo moemeding o o solofetsweng; baakanya nankolo nomore e siametseng ya baemedi baba tla thophiwang; le go nela Tautona nankolo.

(2) Leloko la lekgotla labasekaseki le ekemetseng nka tlosiwa go tswa mo ofising fela ka tshwanelo e amang leloko—

(a)

go leloko le palelwa go diragatsa tiro ya gagwe ka gore bokgoni jwa tlhaloganyo kotsa go dira kamele bosa itikanyela;

(b) ga tlhoka bokgoni go thelele;

(c) ga a a nna le molato; le

(d) ga a satshwanela go tlhophiwa mo le kgotleng le le amiwang.

(3) Tsamaiso ya tloso ya ba atlhodi gotswa kwa ofisini go a ma go tlosiwa go tsweng mo ofisini ya leloko labasekaseki le ekemetseng. KAROLO 2 Lekgotla Labasekaseki Ladi Tlhopho 238 Tshimolodiso le Thamo ya lekgotla labasekaseki ladi tlhopho mo Zimbabwe

(1) Gona lele kgotla la basekaseki ba tlhopho mo Zimbabwe e e na leng—

(a)

modulasetilo ya tlhophilweng ke Tautona morago ga therisano le ga Bodiredi jwa basekaseki jwa dikgotla le Komiti Eming ya Melao le di Taelo; le

(b) maloko a le bofera bobedi a a thamilweng ke Tautona gotsweng nankolong e seng kwatlse ga baemedi ba lesome le bobedi ba neetsweng ke Komiti ya Melao le Taolo e emeng.

(2) Modulasetilo wa lekgotla labasekaseki la Tlhopho la Zimbabwe otshwanetse go na molaodi kgotsa molaodi wapele kgotsa motho yo na le boitseanape jo bo dirang gore a a thamiwe gonna molaodi.

(3) Fa tlhopho ya modulasetilo wa lekgotla labasekaseki ba dithopho go sa fapaane le puelelelo tsa le Bodiredi jwa lekgotla labasekaseki,Tautona otshwanetse go tsaya modi le Komti Eeming ya Melao le Taolo go nna itses/ka keletso ka kgona go direga.

(4) Maloko a a Lekgotla labasekaseki la di Tlhopho atshwanetse go nna moagedi waZimbabwe gape atlhophilwe ka boammaaruri le kitso le ka bokgone ga bonne ka boitshwaro jwa dikgang/merero ga mo rafeng le ga fa tlhokong.

(5) Maloko a a Lekgotla labasekaseki la ditlhopho go Zimbabwe a a athamiwa ga mabaka a a dingwaga tse thataruo gape nka thapiwa gape motswanang go tswelediwa sebaka sengwe salebaka le mme ga gona motho a ka thamaiwang kgotsa go bereka mo le kgotleng labasekaseki morago a a sena gona leloko ga ngwe kgotsa ga bontsi jwa sebaka le fa go tswelediwa kgotsa selekanyo tsa lesome le bobedi ba dingwaga. 239 Tiro ya le kgotlha labasekaseki la ditlhopho mo Zimbabwe Ditiro tsa le Kgotla la basekaseki la Zimbabwe lenna le tiro tse latelang—

(a)

go ipaakanya go itshwara le go tlhokomela—

(i) Ditlhopho mo ofising ya ga Tautona le ga Palamente;

(ii) Ditlhopho ga botleng jwa lefatshe le ga khanseleng tsa toropong golo le dikarolo tsa puso le bolaodi bongwe;

(iii) Ditlhopho tsa maloko ga setshaba ga dikgosi go simolodiwa ga karolo 285; le

(iv) Patlo maikutlo le go tlhomamisa gore ditlhopho tseo le patlo maikutlo a a tsamaiswa sentle ka kgololesego ka fa molaong, ka galalelo go ya ka molao.

(b) go tlhokomela ditlhopho tsa Tautona ga Senate ga sebui le go tlhomamisa gore tsamiso ya ditlhopho tse idirwa sentle le ka fa tshwanelong le molao;

(c)

na nko semmuso ya maina;

(d) ko koanya nankolo ya semmuso ya maina;

(e) go tlhomamisa custody e e tshwanetseng ka tlamelo yanankolo ya semmuso ya maina le;

(f) kganela kgaolwana ya batlhophi kgotla le le mafelo a a seele;

(g) go dira lananeo go gatisa le go kgaoganya di pampiri tsa tlhopho go dumelana semmoso le foreka di bokisi tsa tlhopho le simolodi le go bereka ko matlhophelong;

(h) go tsweledisa le go tlhokomela thuto ya batlhophi;

(i)

(j)

go letleletsa balebeledi ba ditlhopho le patlo maikutlo; go naya bokaedi mo bathong ba ba hirilweng ke goromente kgotsa bolaodi jwa lefelo lengwe ga maikaelelo a a go tlhomamisa go bereka sentle, ka kgololesego ka fa molaong, mo go tshwanetseng le go tsamaisa ditlhopho patlo maikutlo; le

(k) go amogela le go akanya ka kelelelo dingongorego gotswa morefeng le go tsaya kgato mogotshwang kakanyetsong dingongorego jaka go akanywa ka kelelelo mo go siameng. 240 Go kogiwa mo kgaisanong maemo bodireding jwa ditlhopho a a Zimbabwe Matlaleletsa ga batho ba ba umakilweng mo karolong 320(3) ba ba latelang bas a balege go ban eng mo bodireding jwa ditlhopho mo Zimbabwe—

(a)

babereki ba ofisi ya setshaba ba sele ga eseng baatlhodi;

(b) babereki bantlha jwa lefatse le di khansili tsa toropo le balaodi ba kgaolo ngwe lengwe; le

(c)

maloko le babereki badirelang goromente le dingwe tse dilaolwang ke goromente.

241 Lekgotlha laba sekaseki la ditlhopho la Zimbabwe go naya ipego ka ditlhopho le tsa patlo maikutlo Motlaleletsa ga ipego go tlhokagala go ineela ka mabaka a a karolo 323, le kgotlha la ba sekaseki la ditlhopho la Zimbabwele le tshwanetse go ineela go sena tiedo ipego ko Tona etshwanetseng le ineele go palamente ka boitshwaro ja ditlhopho tsotlhe le tsa patlo maikutlo/tlhopho ya le fatse lotlhe ka gopotso nngwe. KAROLO 3 Lekgotla Laba Sekaseki La Ditshwanelo Tsa Setho Mo Zimbabwe 242 Simolodi le thamo la lekgotlha laba sekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho

(1) Gona le lekgotlha laba sekaseki le le etseeng jaka Lekgotlha laba seki seki la Ditshwanelo tsa Setho e nale balatelang—

(a)

modulasetilo yo o thlophilweng ke Tautona morago ga therisano le le badiri ba le kgotlha laba sekaseki ga dikgotlale komiti eming ya laola letaelo; le

(b) maloko mangwe a le boferabobedi a a tlhophilweng ke Tautona gotswa mo nankolong go se palonyana e e kwatlase ga batlhophilweng bale le some le bobedi ba neilweng ke komoti eming ya taolo le taela.

(2) Modulasetilo wa lekgotla laba sekaseki ba ditshwanelo tsa Setso la Zimbabwe yo ona le boitseanape le gale ngwaga tse bosupa a a ikatisajaka diagente tsa Zimbabwe.

(3) Ga a a dira jaka modulasetilo wa le kgotlha la basekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho a a mo go sa fetogeng le puelelo ya bo diredi jwa lekgotla la basekaseki la diagente ona le go baka le komiti e e emeng ya taolo le taela itse gone fela foo ga go kgona godirega.

(4) Maloko a a lekgothla laba sekaseki a a ditshwanelo tsa setho a tshwanetse go tlhophiwa ka buammaaruri le kitso le kutlwisiso le kitso ya bone mo go tlhatlseng ditshwanelo tsa setho. 243 Ditiro tsa lekgotla laba sekaseki le ditshwanelo tsa setho mo Zimbabwe

(1) Lekgotla la ba sekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho la nale ditiro tse latelang—

(a)

Go tlatlosa temogo le tlotlo ya ditshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo ka bodilo jo lekelenang tsa morafe;

(b) Gotlatlosa le go tshereletso thabololo le go fithelela ditshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo;

(c)

Go ela tlhoko, go sekasekale go tlhomamisa kobamelo ditshwanelo tsa setho;

(d) go amogela le go akanya ka kelelelo dingonorego gotswa morafeng le mo go tswang go tsewa dingongorego go akanyetswa ka kelelelo ka tshwanelo;

(e)

go babalela morafe go tshwaragane kgokgontsho ya matla le go sa tsamaisa sentle ga goromente ledi kgwebo tsa morafe ke baookamedi ba dikgwebo tseo;

(f)

go thlomamisa maitshwaro a abolaodi kgotsa motho ga go supiwa gore gingwe ditshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo le go supegetsa mo kitsisongya ditshwanelo go di robilwe ke bolaodi kgotsa motho;

(g) go babalela tsheamo ka tshwanelo go akaretsa, buelela di tshekiso tsa ba dira melato ga go robiwa ditshwanelo tsa setho le gkololo;

(h) go tlhamalatsa Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng wa mapodisi go tlhotlhomisa di tiragalo tsa barukuti ba ba belaelwang. Tlolo molao ga ditshwanelo tsa setho kgotsa kgololole go neela le kgotla labasekaseki felela maduo a a kgaisano ga tlhotlhomiso ye;

(i)

go buelela ba palamente go tsay selkanyo sa go dira ka natla go tlatlosamaemo a a ditshwanelo tsa seth;

(j)

go etelela pele tlhotlhomiso mo di kgatisong tse a mang ditshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo le botsalano; le

(k) go etela le go sekaseka—

(i) kgolegelo le lefelo la tshwaro, kampa ya ba tshabilemo go amang ditlamelo; le

(ii) lefelo ga go nale le bolwetse jwa tlhaloganyo kgotsa go tlhakatlhakanyo;

go botsisisa ka seemo sa batho ba nnang le go dira puelelelo mabapi le seemo se e go Tona e e nang le maikarabelo mo tsamasong molao yo la baganeng le mafelo aa.

(2) Mogolwane yo o tlhophilweng wa Mapodisi otshwanetse go obamela di taelo go umakilwe ke le kgotla labasekaseki ba ditshwanelo tsa setho la Zimbabwe kwatlase ga karolo (1)(h). 244 Pego ga lekgotla laba sekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho mo Zimbabwe

(1) Le Kgotla labasekaseki la ditshwanelo tsa setho la Zimbabwe nka Tlhoka motho mongwe lekgotlha kgotsa kgwebo le fa nnang kgo yo hirilweng ke goromente kgotsa e seng jalo—

(a)

go itseise le kgotla laba sekseki ga selkanyo sa sabonne ba setsere go umakiwa go naya ditlamorago tsa ditshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo go supegetsa kitsiso ya ya phatlhalatsa ga ditshwanelo; le

(b) go tlamela lekgotla laba sekaseki ka kitso etlhokagalanggo bakaanya ipego ngwe e e tljokafalang go ineela kgaolong ngwe kgotsa ga mafatshefatshe kwatlase ga kopano kgolo ya ditshwanelo tsa setho tumalano yamafatshe kgotsa tumalano ga Zimbabwe ele leloko.

(2) Matlalaletso ga ipego gwa tlhokagala go ineela ka mabaka a a karolo 323ga lekgotleng la ditshwanelo tsa setho la Zimbabwe fateng ga tona etshwanetseng ineele ipego ga palamante kakeleleo dikgang tse amanyangdi tshwanelo tsa setho le kgololo efe gamogopolo wa lekgotla laba sekaseki otshwanetseng go atametwa kelotlhoko ya Palamente.

KAROLO 4 Lekgotla La Bong La Zimbabwe 245 Tshimologo le thamo ya lekgotla labasekaseki la bong jwa motho mo Zimbabwe

(1) Go nale lekgotla labasekaseki lemo nna jaka lekgotla la bong jwa mothomo lefatsheng la Zimbabwe ennang le ba ba latelang—

(a)

modulasetilo yo o thamilweng ke Tautona morago ga therisanyo le komiti mabapi le molao le taela; le

(b) maloko a le boferaobedi ba ba—

(i) maloko a a bosupa a a thamilweng ke Tautona gontweng mo nakolong a a seng palonyana ye e kwatlase ga baemedi ba ba lesome le bobedi a neelweng ke komiti e eming ka molao le taela; le

(ii) mongwe wa ba eemedi ba khansili ya di kgosi tsa morafe tse tlhophilweng ke Tautona.

(2) Maloko a a lekgotla la basekaseki ba bong jwa motho ya Zimbabwe tshwanetse go thamiwa ka boammaaruri kitso yabone le bokgoni kutlisiso e e botoka ga boleng jwa motho mo botslanong ga setso go dira le itsholelo le keletsa yasepolotiki le bong jwa motho ba tshwanetse go buelewa ka go lekana mo le kgotleng labasekaseki.

246 Tiro ya lekgotla labasekaseki jwa bo bong jwa motho mo Zimbabwe Lekgotla laba sekaseki ka bong jwa motho jwa bong jwa motho ba Zimbabwe e nnang batelang—

(a)

go ela tlhoko ya kabo go buiwa ka tekatekanyo ya bong jwa motho gotlhotlhomisiwa tekatakanyo ya bong jwa motho jaka tlamelo mo molao motheong;

(b) go tlhotlhomisa go kgonega ga goroba ditshwanelo go amanya bong jwa batho;

(c)

go amogela lego akanya ka kelelo dingongorego tsa morafe le go tsaya kgato mo dingongoregong jaka e e akanyetswa ka tshwanelo;

(d) go etelelela pele patlisiso dikgang tse amanyang bong jwa motho le botsalano le ditsamaiso tsa molao le go fetula dipuelelelo mo molaong, taolo le i katiso efe elatelang pele ga pharologanyong e e kaegileng mo bong jwa motho;

(e)

(f)

go gakolola morafe le makgotla a seele le dikgato tse tshwanetsenggo tsewa le go tlhomamisa tekatekanyo; go buelela/gakolola ka kamogelo kgato le mananeo/thulaganyo go kgna bong jwa motho jo bolekalekanang;

(g) go gakolola ka tshekiso le ga molao ka borukuti le go roba ditshwanelo tse di amang bong jwa motho;

(h) go babalela mogo tshwanetseng fa ditshwanelongtsa bong le fa dirobilweng ka teng; le

(i)

go dira sengwe le sengwe ga konokon go tlhalosa maemo atekanyo ya bong jwa motho.

247 Pego go bapa le lekgotla labasekaseki jwa bong tsa Zimbabwe Gotlaleletsa ipego e e gotlhokagala go ineela sebakeng sa karolo (323) lekgotla labasekaseki la bong mo Zimbabwe ka fetisa tona etshwanetse ineela ipego ko palamente go tlhokometswe kgang e e amayangeng le bong mo mogopolong wa basekaseki e tshwaetseng go tlisa tsiya ya palamente.

KAROLO 5 Lekgotla Labasekaseki La Tsamaiso Di Kgang 248 Simolodi le thamo ya lekgotla laba sekaseki latsamiso dikgang ya Zimbabwe

(1) Gona le lekgotla laba sekaseki la tsamaiso ya dikgang mo Zimbabwe e nnag le—

(a)

modulasetilo ya thlophilweng ke Tautoa morago ga therisano le komiti e eming ka laolo le taelo; le
